### [1] [An Elementary Proof of Hopf's Curvatura Integra Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26294)
*Pavel Grinfeld*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文给出了Hopf曲率积分定理关键方面的初等证明，利用移动曲面微积分证明超曲面的总曲率（Gauss-Kronecker曲率B的曲面积分）在形状光滑变化下保持不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hopf的曲率积分定理是微分几何中的重要结果，该定理表明超曲面的总曲率与形状无关。作者希望提供一个初等证明，使得这一关键结果的证明更加简洁、易于理解，而不是依赖复杂的经典方法。

Method: 采用移动曲面微积分（Calculus of Moving Surfaces）的方法，通过计算总曲率在超曲面形状光滑形变下的变化率，并证明该变化率为零，从而说明总曲率与超曲面形状无关。

Result: 成功给出了Hopf曲率积分定理关键方面的初等证明，证明了总曲率在形状变化下的不变性。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种基于移动曲面微积分的简洁证明路径，为理解Hopf曲率积分定理提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We provide an elementary proof of the key aspect of Hopf's curvatura integra theorem. Namely, we show that the total curvature, i.e. the surface integral of the Gauss-Kronecker curvature B, is independent of the shape of the hypersurface. We accomplish this task by using the Calculus of Moving Surfaces to show that the rate of change of the total curvature vanishes under smooth changes in shape.

</details>


### [2] [A Horizon-Free Extrinsic Penrose Inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26565)
*Caiyan Li*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了三维欧氏空间中具有有限个端的、正常嵌入的、平均凸平面曲面S的一个几何不等式：S的渐近平端外部质量m_+(S)不小于√(A_S/π)，其中A_S是分离该端与其余所有端的紧致曲面面积的下确界。该不等式不要求最外层自由边界最小曲面假设，对其余端也无渐近或可积性条件。文章还刻画了等号成立的情形：A_S=0时恰为欧氏半平面；完整悬链面等号成立且A_S>0；若A_S>0且等号成立，则A_S由朝向指定端的、平坦的自由边界圆盘取到，且S的对应分支为半悬链面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在几何测度论与广义相对论/几何分析的交叉领域，研究者希望建立平均凸曲面外部质量与其拓扑/面积不变量之间的定量关系。此前的相关结果通常假设存在最外层自由边界最小曲面或对曲面整体施加严格条件，这限制了应用范围。本文旨在去除这些限制，证明一个更一般且更简洁的不等式，并完整刻画等号情形，从而深化对平均凸平面曲面（如悬链面）几何结构的理解。

Method: 本文通过选取曲面S的一个渐近平端，定义外部质量m_+(S)（在H_S在该端可积的前提下），并引入A_S——分离指定端与其余所有端的紧致曲面的最小面积下确界。作者利用平均凸性构造适当的测试曲面/单位分解，结合曲面的几何变分与比较原理，推导出质量下界不等式。在等号分析中，利用A_S的达成性、自由边界最小曲面的稳定性和刚性问题，将曲面的对应分支识别为半悬链面或欧氏半平面。

Result: 证明了不等式m_+(S) ≥ √(A_S/π)，该结果不依赖最外层自由边界最小曲面假设，也不对其余端施加限制。等号刻画：A_S=0时，恰为欧氏半平面；完整悬链面给出A_S>0时的一个等号例子；若A_S>0且等号成立，则A_S由朝向指定端的平坦自由边界圆盘取到，且S的对应分支是半悬链面。

Conclusion: 该结果建立了一个干净且一般的外质量-面积不等式，将所有已知特殊情况（包括悬链面）纳入统一框架，并给出了完整的等号分类。这为平均凸平面曲面的几何刚性理论提供了新的工具，同时暗示相关方法可推广到更高维或更一般的平均凸嵌入曲面情形。

Abstract: Let $S\subset\mathbb R^3$ be a properly embedded mean-convex planar surface with finitely many ends. Designate one end as asymptotically flat, assume that $H_S$ is integrable there, and denote its extrinsic mass by $m_+(S)$. Let $A_S$ be the infimum of the areas of compact surfaces separating the distinguished end from all the others. We prove \[ m_+(S)\geq\sqrt{\frac{A_S}π}. \] No outermost free-boundary minimal surface is assumed, and no asymptotic or integrability condition is imposed on the other ends. If $A_S=0$, equality holds precisely for the Euclidean half-space. The complete catenoid realizes equality with $A_S>0$. Conversely, if equality holds with $A_S>0$, then $A_S$ is attained by a flat free-boundary disk that is outermost toward the distinguished end, and the corresponding component of $S$ is a half-catenoid.

</details>


### [3] [Pinching cones for positive isotropic curvature in dimensions seven and eight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26598)
*Jae Ho Cho*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文构建了n=7和n=8维Hamilton ODE（dR/dt=Q(R)）的横向不变钉扎锥族，将Brendle（n≥12）和Chen（9≤n≤11）的钉扎估计推广到更低维度。针对Chen构造在n=8失效的两个步骤（第二锥族的耦合估计和锥族粘合），以及n=7失效的端点约简和平均族论证，分别通过保留耦合项、将粘合约化为单一线性不等式并利用有限框不等式证明、重新平衡参数、直接多项式正定证书和第一Bianchi恒等式等方法加以修复。最终结合Brendle的无维度论证得到n=7,8维正各向同性曲率紧致流形的拓扑分类，并联合Cho-Li与Brendle-Naff的曲率改进和分类结果，得到一致PIC非紧kapp-非坍缩古解的完整分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在将Brendle和Chen关于闭流形正各向同性曲率（PIC）分类结果的维度范围从n≥9（Brendle的n≥12和Chen的9≤n≤11）推广到更低维度n=7和n=8。这一维度上的推广并非平凡的延伸，因为Chen构造中的关键步骤在n=8时真正失效，需要全新的论证方法。

Method: 作者采用以下方法：(1) 对n=8，修复Chen第二锥族估计中丢弃的耦合项；将锥族粘合问题约化为圆锥弱PIC曲率算子上的单一线性不等式，并通过有限个框不等式的显式组合证明之。(2) 对n=7，重新平衡两个锥族参数，用直接多项式正定证书替代端点约简，用基于第一Bianchi恒等式的不等式替代粘合证书中的平均族部分。最终结合Brendle的无维度论证以及Cho-Li和Brendle-Naff的曲率改进结果完成分类。

Result: 成功在n=7,8维度构建了横截不变钉扎锥族，获得对应维度的钉扎估计，从而完成：(1) 含无平凡不可压缩(n-1)维空间形式的紧致正各向同性曲率流形的拓扑分类（将Brendle的定理从n≥12推广到n=7,8）；(2) 一致PIC、kappa-非坍缩的非紧古解的分类（将Cho-Li的结果从n=4或n≥12推广到n=7,8）。

Conclusion: 本文通过精密的维度特异性论证填补了n=7和n=8的空白，表明该维度中Chen的通用构造虽然失效，但可以借助更细致的耦合保留、线性不等式约化、参数重新平衡和基于Bianchi恒等式的新不等式来修复。这完成了Ricci流下PIC流形分类的低维推广，丰富了Brendle-Chen框架的适用范围。

Abstract: We construct families of transversally invariant pinching cones for the Hamilton ODE dR/dt = Q(R) in dimensions n=7,8, thereby extending the pinching estimate that Brendle established for n >= 12 and Chen for 9 <= n <= 11. Two of the steps in Chen's construction genuinely fail at n=8. The first, an estimate for the second cone family, is repaired by keeping a coupling that is discarded in the dimension-general argument. The second is the gluing of the two cone families, for which Chen's estimate fails at n=8 by a definite margin. We replace it by a new argument which reduces the gluing to a single linear inequality on the cone of weakly PIC curvature operators, and proves that inequality by an explicit finite combination of frame inequalities. At n=7 two further steps break down: the endpoint reduction behind the first cone family, and part of the averaging family behind our gluing certificate. After re-balancing the parameters of both cone families, we replace the former by direct polynomial positivity certificates and the latter by inequalities resting on the first Bianchi identity. The resulting pinching estimate, together with dimension-free arguments of Brendle, yields in dimensions seven and eight the topological classification of compact manifolds with positive isotropic curvature that contain no nontrivial incompressible (n-1)-dimensional space forms, extending a theorem of Brendle from n >= 12. Together with the curvature-improvement and classification results of Cho-Li and Brendle-Naff, it yields the classification of noncompact kappa-noncollapsed ancient solutions to the Ricci flow with uniformly PIC, extending a theorem of Cho-Li from n=4 or n >= 12.

</details>


### [4] [Uniqueness of positively curved ancient Ricci flows on surfaces with boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26619)
*Kyeongho Bang,Eric Chen,Wenkui Du*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文首次建立了带边二维曲面上正曲率古Ricci流的分类定理，证明在均匀有界直径和边界测地曲率为正常数的条件下，该古流在时间无关微分同胚意义下存在且唯一，并具有旋转对称性、后向极限为平坦圆盘、前向极限为半球奇点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 古Ricci流是Ricci流研究中的重要课题，但以往的分类结果主要针对无边流形。带边界的情况在几何分析中具有广泛应用（如带边曲面的几何演化），然而此前缺乏对带边古Ricci流的分类定理。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立带边情形下的首个分类结果。

Method: 本文研究了二维带边曲面(M^2, ∂M^2, g(t))上的古Ricci流，假设直径均匀有界且边界测地曲率为正常数。通过建立存在性和唯一性（模去时间无关微分同胚），并利用解的对称性分析、后向极限与前述极限的几何结构分析来完成分类论证。

Result: 证明了带边二维曲面上正曲率古Ricci流的存在唯一性（模时间无关微分同胚），所得解具有旋转对称性；后向极限为平坦圆盘，前向极限为半球奇点。这是带边古Ricci流分类问题的首个结果。

Conclusion: 该结果开创了带边古Ricci流分类理论的先河，揭示了带边正曲率古Ricci流的几何刚性特征：旋转对称性与极限结构的完全刻画，为后续更高维或更一般边界条件下的古Ricci流分类研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We establish the existence and uniqueness modulo time-independent diffeomorphisms of the positively curved ancient Ricci flow $(M^2, \partial M^2, g(t))$ on a two-dimensional surface with boundary, assuming uniformly bounded diameter and constant positive boundary geodesic curvature. In particular, this ancient Ricci flow is rotationally symmetric, its backward limit is the flat disk, and its forward limit is a half-spherical singularity. To our knowledge, this result is the first instance of a classification result for ancient Ricci flows with boundary.

</details>


### [5] [Extrinsic upper bounds for the harmonic mean of eigenvalues in curved manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26723)
*Hang Chen,Yuna Gao*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文给出了流形M上拉普拉斯算子前m个非零特征值的调和平均的外在（extrinsic）上界，其中M是截面曲率上有界（上界为δ）的黎曼流形中的m维闭定向子流形，推广了此前关于第一个非零特征值的相关结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在黎曼几何中，特征值估计是核心研究课题之一。已有研究给出了子流形上第一个非零特征值的外在上界，但关于前m个非零特征值（特别是其调和平均）的研究尚不充分。本文旨在将此前的第一特征值结果推广到前m个非零特征值的调和平均，从而获得更一般的特征值估计。

Method: 本文研究了截面曲率上界为δ的黎曼流形中的m维闭定向子流形M，通过调和分析、谱几何和子流形几何的方法，推导出M上拉普拉斯算子前m个非零特征值的调和平均的外在上界。

Result: 获得了闭定向子流形上拉普拉斯算子前m个非零特征值的调和平均的外在上界，该结果推广了此前针对第一个非零特征值的相关结论。

Conclusion: 本文成功将第一非零特征值的外在上界结果推广至前m个非零特征值的调和平均，为子流形上的高阶特征值估计提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Let $M$ be an $m (\ge2)$-dimensional closed orientable submanifold in a Riemannian manifold of sectional curvature bounded above by $δ$.
  We obtain the extrinsic upper bounds of the harmonic mean of the first $m$ nonzero eigenvalues of the Laplacian on $M$, which extend the previous related results for the first nonzero eigenvalues.

</details>


### [6] [On bubbling of Fueter maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26803)
*Jacek Rzemieniecki*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究Hamiltonian扰动的Fueter方程解序列的bubbling现象。在Walpuski紧致性定理下，有界能量序列弱收敛到Fueter映射，能量损失由codimension-2可整流集上的缺陷测度记录。假设极限映射无不可去奇点、bubbling轨迹含非平凡Lipschitz弧且气泡沿该弧附着于极限映射，则极限Hamiltonian属于无穷余维的例外子集。证明结合超Kähler流形中有理扫描轨迹的定量控制与横截性论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Hamiltonian扰动Fueter方程解序列的bubbling现象在何种条件下是本质的（generic）还是非本质的（nongeneric）。通过证明在无不可去奇点假设下bubbling高度非典型，揭示该方程的解结构的稳定性与刚性。

Method: 结合两个关键技术：(1) 超Kähler流形中有理扫描轨迹（rational sweepout loci）的定量控制；(2) 横截性（transversality）论证。作者证明在Lipschitz弧假设和气泡附着假设下，极限Hamiltonian必须落入一个无穷余维的例外集合，从而排除bubbling的普遍性。

Result: 证明了：在极限映射无不可去奇点、bubbling轨迹含非平凡Lipschitz弧、气泡沿弧附着这三条假设下，极限Hamiltonian属于无穷余维的例外子集。这表明Hamiltonian扰动Fueter映射的bubbling在无不可去奇点情况下高度非典型（nongeneric）。

Conclusion: 该结果强烈暗示，在没有不可去奇点的情形下，Hamiltonian扰动Fueter映射的bubbling是高度非典型的。作者预期Lipschitz弧假设和附着假设均为自动满足的，这进一步支持bubbling在这类问题中的非本质性。

Abstract: We study bubbling for sequences of solutions of the Hamiltonian-perturbed Fueter equation. By Walpuski's compactness theorem, a bounded-energy sequence converges weakly to a Fueter map, with energy loss recorded by a defect measure supported on a codimension-two rectifiable set. Assuming that the limiting map has no non-removable singularities, the bubbling locus contains a nontrivial Lipschitz arc and the bubbles attach to the limiting map along this arc, we show that the limiting Hamiltonian lies in an exceptional subset of infinite codimension. The proof combines quantitative control of rational sweepout loci in hyperkähler manifolds with a transversality argument. We expect both the Lipschitz-arc and attachment assumptions to be automatic. This strongly suggests that, in the absence of non-removable singularities, bubbling of Hamiltonian-perturbed Fueter maps is highly nongeneric.

</details>


### [7] [On the existence of maximal foliations in general relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26816)
*Bing-Long Chen,Ruo-Bin Wu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文针对非极值初始超曲面的情况下，在满足真空爱因斯坦约束方程且平均曲率满足小性条件的渐近平坦柯西曲面附近，构造极大叶化（maximal foliations）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极大叶化是求解爱因斯坦方程的关键规范条件，在闵可夫斯基时空全局稳定性和L^2曲率有界猜想中发挥了核心作用。然而当初始超曲面不是极值的（即平均曲率不为零）时，传统的椭圆方法在构造极大叶化时会遇到根本性困难。本文旨在克服这一困难，为一般初始数据提供极大叶化的构造方案。

Method: 通过对初始渐近平坦柯西曲面施加一个关于平均曲率的适当小性条件，作者提供了在该初始曲面附近构造极大叶化的方法，从而绕开了传统椭圆方法在处理非极值初始超曲面时遇到的本质困难。

Result: 在满足真空爱因斯坦约束方程且平均曲率满足适当小性条件的任何渐近平坦初始柯西曲面附近，成功构造出极大叶化。

Conclusion: 这项工作将极大叶化的构造从极值初始超曲面推广到了一类满足小平均曲率条件的非极值初始数据，为更广泛地应用极大叶化这一规范条件求解爱因斯坦方程奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: It is well known that the Einstein equations are tensor equations for a Lorentzian metric. Hence, choosing a suitable gauge condition is crucial for solving them. As a powerful gauge condition, maximal foliations have played a pivotal role in two groundbreaking works in general relativity: the global stability of Minkowski spacetime and the bounded L^2 curvature conjecture. Nevertheless, if the initial hypersurface fails to be maximal, the usual elliptic approach for constructing maximal foliations encounters fundamental difficulties when solving the Einstein equations.
  The purpose of the paper is to provide a construction of maximal foliations around any initial asymptotically flat Cauchy surface satisfying vacuum Einstein constraint equations, under a suitable smallness condition on the mean curvature.

</details>


### [8] [Evolutes, involutes and parallels of Legendre curves in hyperbolic and de Sitter 2-spaces via rotational transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26823)
*Nozomi Nakatsuyama,Masatomo Takahashi,Anjie Zhou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的方法，通过旋转变换（旋转、boost和零旋转）来构造Legendre曲线的渐屈线、渐伸线和平行线的移动标架，并系统地给出了双曲2空间和de Sitter 2空间中这些曲线的定义，讨论了它们之间的联系与对偶性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 渐屈线、渐伸线和平行线是经典微分几何中的重要概念，但以往的研究通常针对不同空间和不同曲线类型分别处理，缺乏统一的构造框架。本文旨在通过旋转变换建立统一方法，以便在双曲空间和de Sitter空间中系统处理这些曲线。

Method: 本文采用统一的旋转变换框架（包括旋转、boost和零旋转三类变换），利用Legendre曲线的移动标架，通过三类旋转变换分别构造渐屈线、渐伸线和平行线的移动标架，并在双曲2空间与de Sitter 2空间中系统地给出这些曲线的定义。

Result: 系统地建立了双曲2空间和de Sitter 2空间中Legendre曲线的渐屈线、渐伸线和平行线的定义，并通过统一旋转变换方法构造了相应的移动标架，揭示了这些曲线之间的内在联系与对偶关系。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种统一的数学框架，能够同时在双曲和de Sitter 2空间中处理Legendre曲线的渐屈线、渐伸线和平行线，并通过旋转变换建立了这些经典曲线概念之间的联系，为后续研究提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We develop a unified approach to constructing the moving frames of the evolute, involute and parallel of a Legendre curve via rotational transformations (rotation, boost and null rotation). The definitions of the evolute, involute and parallel of a Legendre curve in hyperbolic and de Sitter 2-spaces are systematically presented. Furthermore, the connections and dualities among these curves are discussed.

</details>


### [9] [Finite semisimplicial sets, differential graded algebras, and cellular sheaves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26953)
*Jan-Willem Van Looy,Ferdinando Zanchetta*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文构建了有限非奇异半单纯集及其胞腔层的外微分DG模型（exterior DGA Ω^•_S），将有限简单有向图与一阶微分演算之间的经典反等价推广到更高维度。作者刻画了该构造的本质象，得到与图-FODC对应在维数1时恢复的范畴等价；对一个固定的S，刻画了一类与S上胞腔层等价的Ω^•_S-模范畴。最后研究了该框架中的联络与曲率：联络由边线性映射描述，2维单形上的曲率是直接边传输与复合边传输之差，当边传输可逆时曲率处处消失等价于将边传输延拓为P_S上联络层的粘合判据。

Motivation: 经典工作中，有限简单有向图与一阶微分演算之间存在反等价关系。本文旨在将该对应推广到更高阶/高维组合对象（即有限非奇异半单纯集）上，并进一步将微分结构引入胞腔层的描述，从而获得更丰富的几何/拓扑工具。

Abstract: We develop a differential graded model for finite non-singular semisimplicial sets and their cellular sheaves. To a finite non-singular semisimplicial set \(S\), we associate an exterior DGA \(Ω^\bullet_S\), extending the classical anti-equivalence between finite simple directed graphs and first-order differential calculi to higher degrees. We characterize the essential image of this construction, obtaining an equivalence of categories that recovers the graph-FODC correspondence in dimension one. For a fixed \(S\), we then characterize a category of differential graded \(Ω^\bullet_S\)-modules equivalent to the category of cellular sheaves on $S$, thereby giving a differential refinement of the usual incidence-algebra description. Finally, we study connections and curvature in this framework. Connections are described by edgewise linear maps and their curvature on a 2-dimensional simplex is the difference between direct and composite edge transport. When the edge transports are invertible, vanishing of this curvature on every \(2\)-simplex can equivalently be seen as a gluing criterion to extend the given edge transports to a connection sheaf on \(P_S\).

Abstract: It is not a priori clear which of the Gauduchon connections is better suited to LCK and Vaisman manifolds. We thus investigate the geometry of these connections through Einstein problems and more general analytic and cohomological conditions on their Ricci tensors. Our results single out the Bismut connection as the privileged one for the non-Kähler lcK and Vaisman geometry. We therefore study the {second-Bismut--Einstein} equation and provide a characterization of these structures on Hopf manifolds.

Abstract: We show that the spacelike solutions to the curvature flow for curves on the De Sitter space are in correspondence with the solutions to the inverse curvature flow on the 2-dimensional hyperbolic space, that on the De Sitter space, the timelike solutions to the curvature flow are in correspondence with the timelike solutions to the inverse curvature flow, and that the solutions curve shortening flow on the 2-dimensional hyperbolic space are in correspondence with the solutions to the spacelike solutions to the inverse curvature flow on the De Sitter space. We prove that, for spacelike curves on the De Sitter space, the curvature flow is a gradient-type flow for the arc-length functional. We observe that a spacelike or timelike curve on the De Sitter space is a soliton solution to the curvature flow (resp. inverse curvature flow) if and only if its curvature (resp. inverse of its curvature) can be written as the inner product between its tangent vector field and a fixed vector $v$ of the 3-dimensional Minkowski space. We prove that for each vector $v$, there exists a 2-parameter family of timelike (spacelike) soliton solutions to the curvature flow and to the inverse curvature flow on the De Sitter space. We show that there exists no non-trivial complete timelike soliton. There exist non-trivial complete spacelike solutions. As a consequence of curvature flow, we obtain the behavior of the soliton solutions to the inverse curvature flow on the De Sitter space and hyperbolic space.

Abstract: Let $\mathbf{C}=\partial E\subset \mathbb{R}^{n+1}$ be a regular area-minimizing hypercone. We first prove that $\mathbf{C}$ is simultaneously strictly stable and strictly minimizing if and only if there exists $c_\mathbf{C}>0$ such that \[
  \operatorname{Per}(F;B_R)-\operatorname{Per}(E;B_R)
  \geq
  c_\mathbf{C}
  \int_{F\mathbin\triangle E}
  \frac{\operatorname{dist}(x,\mathbf{C})}{|x|^2}\,dx \] for every $R>0$ and finite-perimeter set $F$ with $F\mathbin\triangle E\Subset B_R$. Thus this intrinsic distance-weighted inequality characterizes exactly the simultaneous strictness of the stability and minimizing properties. Second, without either strictness assumption, every regular area-minimizing hypercone satisfies the scale-invariant quadratic inequality \[
  \frac{\operatorname{Per}(F;B_R)-\operatorname{Per}(E;B_R)}{R^n}
  \geq
  c_\mathbf{C}
  \left(
  \frac{|F\mathbin\triangle E|}{R^{n+1}}
  \right)^2. \] This extends the inequality previously established for area-minimizing Lawson cones. Finally, the area-minimizing assumption is unnecessary for our spectral result: for every stable regular minimal hypercone, the first Dirichlet eigenvalue $λ_\mathbf{C}^D(R)$ of the Jacobi operator on $\mathbf{C}\cap B_R$ is given exactly by \[
  λ_\mathbf{C}^D(R)
  =
  \frac{j_{b_1,1}^2}{R^2},
  \qquad
  b_1^2
  =
  \frac{(n-2)^2}{4}+μ_1, \] where $μ_1$ is the first eigenvalue of the link Jacobi operator and $j_{b_1,1}$ is the first positive zero of the Bessel function $J_{b_1}$. In particular, this identifies the optimal Dirichlet spectral constant for every stable regular minimal hypercone.

</details>

As a consequence, we establish the sharp \(2\)-systole estimate for every positive scalar curvature Kähler manifold $(X,ω)$ and prove $\min_XS(ω) \cdot\operatorname{sys}_2(ω) \le 2πr(r+1)$, where \(r\) is the rational dimension of $X$, with equality if and only if the universal cover splits as $(\widetilde X,\widetilde ω) \cong (\mathbb P^r,ω_{\mathrm{FS}}) \times(Y^{n-r},ω_{\mathrm{RF}})$ up to normalization where $ω_{\mathrm{FS}}$ is the Fubini-Study metric and $ω_{\mathrm{RF}}$ is Ricci-flat. With the newer Einstein-delay implementation, the total disagreement (over one year) drops to $\stddtau\lesssim\SI{31}{\nano\second}$ (or $μ\lesssim\num{4e-8}$ at $f=\SI{1000}{\hertz}$) and is dominated by the observatory contribution to the \Romer{} delay, owing to the approximate Earth-rotation model used by \lalsuite{}. Abstract: We investigate static spherically symmetric vacuum solutions in higher-dimensional $f(R)$ gravity, beginning with the five-dimensional Starobinsky model governed by the action $f(R) = R + αR^2 - 2Λ$. Generalizing this analysis to arbitrary dimensions $D$ and single-term curvature corrections $f(R) = R + αR^n$ with a vanishing bare cosmological constant, we derive a universal stability bound, $n > D/2$, required for the existence of stable emergent vacua. Finally, we demonstrate that expanding the gravitational action to a multi-term polynomial hierarchy circumvents this strict limitation. By including curvature corrections up to $\mathcal{O}(R^3)$, the extended geometric degrees of freedom simultaneously satisfy the trace constraint and the stability criterion. Furthermore, we establish the exact parameter space boundaries that ensure not only asymptotic vacuum stability but strict global stability ($f'(R) > 0$ for all $R$), allowing for the dynamical generation of exact, globally ghost-free vacuum spacetimes in $D \ge 5$ purely from higher-order geometric terms. The scalaron mass requirement, $m_s^2>0$ is not imposed in full in our analysis. A detailed investigation of its implications on the models considered in this work are left for future study.

Abstract: We prove the Arnold-Givental conjecture in full generality: given a closed symplectic manifold $(X, ω)$, an anti-symplectic involution $τ_X: X \to X$ with fixed point set $L={\rm Fix}(τ_X)$, and a Hamiltonian diffeomorphism $φ: X \to X$ such that $φ(L)$ intersects transversely with $L$, the following inequality holds: \[ \# \big( φ(L) \cap L \big) \geq {\mathrm dim}_{{\mathbb F}_2} H_*(L; {\mathbb F}_2).\] The proof combines the methods of integral Floer theory of the first and fourth authors, a reduction to Hamiltonian Floer cohomology due to Lu, and a new idea related to localization in a $\mathbb Z/2$-equivariant Floer theory tailored to the problem.

Abstract: Counting processes provide a fundamental description of stochastic events ranging from photon detection to clock ticks. A central question is how accurately such events can be timed when only finite memory resources are available. For the qubit case, we further derive an analytical large-mean bound within a single-Kraus no-tick family, showing that the quantum advantage persists asymptotically within this class. The central claim is that observational facts must be indexed to perspectives. A statement such as "the friend saw outcome A" is not a complete factual statement within Convivial Solipsism. It must be reformulated as "from the friend's perspective, the friend saw outcome A" or "from Wigner's perspective, the friend reported outcome B." The mistake consists in removing these indices and then treating the resulting expressions as if they belonged to a single global factual domain. This leads to apparent contradictions and to false dilemmas concerning consciousness, communication, other minds, and intersubjectivity. Increasing the excitation number $N$ yields a family of protocols that violates CHSH for $η>η_G=(\sqrt{5}-1)/2$, with the asymptotic golden ratio approached as $N \to \infty$. Finally, we present a sparse-deletion binomial PI protocol that guarantees CHSH violation for up to $O(\sqrt n)$ deletion errors. Our results distinguish fundamental limits imposed by loss from those set by explicit measurements without recovery.

The impact of memory is intrinsically state-dependent. Abstract: The physics of information scrambling in quantum many-body systems is intimately related to thermalisation and emergence of chaos. However, its standard characterisation through bipartite entanglement or operator growth remains inherently coarse-grained, obscuring the spatiotemporal anatomy of how quantum information flows between different regions of the system and across various length scales. In this work, we develop a theory for the dynamics of local information in local random unitary circuits which resolves the fine-grained microscopic structures of local information flow in such systems. Using the framework of information lattice which systematises the information content at each length scale within each subsystem, we identify a distribution of length scales at which information resides and obtain the dynamics of the distribution. For Haar-random circuits with infinite local Hilbert-space dimensions, we map this dynamics onto an exact classical stochastic process which reveals that this distribution is described by a Tracy-Widom form which moves ballistically in time ($ \propto t$) toward larger scales, accompanied by a $\sim t^{1/3}$ broadening. We also find the same qualitative behaviour for random Clifford circuits acting on qubits. The similar scaling behaviour in two rather different settings hints strongly towards the universality of our results. In the case of Clifford circuits, we develop a phenomenological Markov process for the dynamics of the stabiliser generators in a specific gauge which confirms the Tracy-Widom distribution and the $t^{1/3}$ scaling of the fluctuations. Ultimately, our results establish the universal properties of the dynamics of local quantum information in a length scale-resolved fashion and provide a possible route towards bridging exact microscopic theories for information dynamics with emergent hydrodynamic descriptions of information flow.

Abstract: Cloud quantum processors compile submitted quantum error correction circuits and may colocate them with untrusted workloads. Abstract: The realization of large-scale quantum networks requires more than advances in quantum repeaters, memories, and processors, it requires a framework explaining how heterogeneous quantum technologies evolve from isolated deployments into interconnected infrastructures. While the classical Internet evolved under strong utility incentives associated with resource sharing and communication demands, quantum networking currently lacks dominant applications capable of generating comparable incentives. As a consequence, contemporary quantum networks are largely formed through technology-driven decisions motivated by technical feasibility, experimental validation, and expected future value. This work argues that the absence of utility-driven network formation is not solely a consequence of immature applications, but also of insufficient abstraction. In particular, heterogeneous quantum platforms remain tightly coupled to the functionalities they provide, preventing the definition of technology-independent utility functions. A hierarchical architecture consisting of Physical Platforms (PP), Functionalities (F), Services (S), Applications (A), and Use-Cases (UC) is proposed, together with the argument that quantum interconnects constitute the enabling technology required to decouple physical implementations from network functionalities. Such decoupling permits the definition of utility functions at the functionality level and establishes the conditions under which strategic (agent-based) network formation becomes applicable. Quantum interconnects should therefore be viewed not only as interoperability devices, but also as fundamental enablers of strategic quantum network evolution.

In the degree-only circuit, $78.05\%$ of selected $p=1$ shots realize the invalid disconnected ground state; the reduced 14-qubit flow-augmented circuit yields no fully feasible sample. These device results characterize mapped Hamiltonians and compilation rather than an asymptotic routing solution advantage.

Abstract: High-frequency squeezed states are important for quantum metrology and information processing, but quadrature measurements at gigahertz frequencies remain challenging. Balanced homodyne detection (BHD), the standard approach, requires closely matched complex transfer functions in both photodetection channels to suppress local-oscillator (LO) noise and maintain a well-defined readout quadrature. This matching becomes increasingly difficult at high bandwidths. Strongly asymmetric single-photodiode homodyne detection avoids this requirement, but achieving high signal efficiency and shot-noise clearance typically requires hundreds of milliwatts of LO power and does not suppress technical LO sidebands. I propose cavity-assisted homodyne detection, in which the quantum field and LO enter separate ports of an impedance-matched traveling-wave cavity. At the same time, technical LO sidebands outside the cavity linewidth are suppressed. I derive the quantum input-output relations and linearized photocurrent including intracavity loss, and show that near-unity signal-transfer efficiency can be achieved at moderate incident LO power. Efficient execution of quantum algorithms on DQC architectures requires compiling them across QPUs while minimizing inter-QPU communication bottlenecks, primarily through circuit partitioning. However, current evaluations of state-of-the-art partitioning heuristics focus primarily on the total entanglement cost of the partitions, failing to capture the broader structural and temporal overheads introduced by distributed network constraints. This paper addresses this evaluation gap by applying established monolithic benchmarking metrics to partitioned distributed circuits to quantify the performance impact of network constraints. Using an open-source, automated evaluation pipeline, we systematically assess diverse partitioning algorithms across standardized workloads and quantum network topologies. Our empirical results reveal that partitioning algorithms with comparable entanglement costs can still introduce drastically different physical execution penalties. By exposing these hidden trade-offs, such as severe increases in circuit depth and substantial reductions in gate density, this study demonstrates that comprehensive circuit-level metrics are essential for guiding the future design of DQC compilers.

This results in an effective spectral density that is a collection of shifted bare spectra arising from the non-equidistant energy gaps of the anharmonic environment, along with a unique zero-frequency term that effectively acts as static disorder on the system. This is due to the non-zero variance of the diagonal of the bath coupling operator. For both continuous and discrete damped anharmonic environments, this work investigates their unique influence on the system's dynamics, including enhanced non-Markovianity compared to a harmonic approximation, novel effects in the absorption spectrum, and the potential for anharmonic environments to enhance energy transport.

Abstract: Contraction coefficients quantify the loss of distinguishability induced by a channel and provide a strong form of the data-processing inequality. While standard contraction coefficients ignore auxiliary quantum systems, existing extensions based on complete contraction coefficients require the compared states to have identical reference marginals. In this work, we introduce conditional contraction coefficients, a novel family that incorporates arbitrary quantum reference systems by subtracting the distinguishability already present in the reference system. We develop a general framework for contraction coefficients with such quantum side information, including the corresponding strong-data-processing-inequality (SDPI) constants, expansion coefficients, and relative contraction coefficients. For the trace distance, we show that the optimization can be restricted to orthogonal input states. For the quantum relative entropy, we prove that its conditional contraction coefficient is exactly equal to the contraction coefficient of the conditional mutual information, extending the classical correspondence between relative-entropy contraction and mutual-information contraction to the setting with quantum side information. More generally, we identify structural properties of divergences required for these results and discuss extensions beyond the relative entropy. These results establish a unified framework for analyzing information contraction in quantum network settings, where quantum side information and distributed correlations are intrinsic features of the information-processing task. Applications include an extension of the Polyanskiy-Wu bounds on mutual information contraction, new perspectives on mixing times, and fundamental limits on quantum memories.

</details>

Abstract: We determine the strong converse exponent for the entanglement cost of quantum state merging, showing that it is characterized by the optimized $α$-$z$ conditional Rényi entropies with $z=α/2\in[1/2,1]$. As a consequence, we derive the strong converse exponent of the partially smoothed conditional min-entropy in purified distance. Abstract: Coherent measurements across multiple copies of an unknown quantum state can substantially reduce the number of samples required to learn its properties, but remain experimentally challenging. Current experiments typically prepare and measure one copy at a time, potentially adapting later measurements to earlier outcomes. A central challenge is adaptivity, which makes the space of possible measurement strategies difficult to characterize. The positive-partial-transpose (PPT) relaxation bypasses this complexity by considering a larger, mathematically tractable class of measurements, at the risk of weakening the resulting bounds. Here we show, surprisingly, that the relaxation loses nothing at the level of asymptotic sample complexity for two fundamental tasks: purity testing and product testing. In both cases, lower bounds against the full class of PPT measurements are matched by nonadaptive single-copy protocols. Moreover, our proof requires only basic symmetric subspace identities, providing a simple route to sharp lower bounds for adaptive single-copy measurements.

</details>

Abstract: Single photon emitters in silicon carbide (SiC) are a leading platform for scalable quantum technologies. Recent interest has focused on oxygen-vacancy-related emitters, which show exceptionally high optical brightness and strong spin readout contrast. One barrier to scalable quantum devices based on these emitters is the challenge of maskless formation and rapid identification. Here, we demonstrate the formation of isolated bright single emitters in 4H-SiC, using low-energy maskless implantation of heavy ions bismuth and tin. Following annealing, up to 18% of implanted sites host a single emitter, with optimal yields achieved at annealing temperatures of 900-1000 degrees C. Occupancy statistics are modelled to estimate the implantation dose that maximises single-emitter yield. We introduce a tiered characterisation scheme, where a simple intensity threshold isolates single-emitter candidates, confirmed through photon correlation measurements, after which correlations between polarisation, saturation count rate and magnetic resonance frequency assign emitter type. It is shown that time-consuming low-temperature spectroscopy is not necessary to distinguish emitter types. Together, maskless heavy-ion implantation and selective screening offer an efficient route to forming and rapidly identifying near-surface single emitters for room-temperature quantum technologies such as quantum sensing.

*Marco Fanizza,Dmitry Grinko,Thilo Scharnhorst,Jack Spilecki*

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子态的近似克隆和近似转置问题，证明了混合态克隆的下界：要克隆k份未知秩为r的混合量子态至保真度1-ε，至少需要n = Ω(krd/ε)份输入副本，这与随机纯化信道给出的上界相匹配，从而完全刻画了混合态克隆的资源复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Werner的纯态克隆机表明纯态克隆需要n = Θ(kd/ε)份副本，而随机纯化信道将其自然推广到混合态场景，给出n = O(krd/ε)的上界。核心问题在于：能否做得更好？即混合态克隆是否存在更优的信道？本文旨在证明该上界是最优的（即匹配下界），填补混合态克隆复杂度刻画中的空白。

Method: 本文通过研究投影克隆（projector cloning）这一特殊情形来证明下界：输入态ρ被承诺为P/r形式，其中P是秩为r的正交投影。通过分析投影克隆的难度来推导一般混合态克隆的下界。此外，将相同技术应用于近似转置问题（将ρ^{⊗n}转换为(ρ^T)^{⊗k}的高保真近似态），同样证明其需要n = Θ(krd/ε)份副本。

Result: 证明了混合态克隆的下界为n = Ω(krd/ε)，与随机纯化信道给出的上界n = O(krd/ε)相匹配，即混合态克隆的副本复杂度为Θ(krd/ε)。同时，将该技术应用于近似转置问题，也证明了其最优副本复杂度为Θ(krd/ε)。

Conclusion: 混合态近似克隆无法超越随机纯化信道方案的性能，其副本复杂度为n = Θ(krd/ε)，与秩r相关（纯态时r=1退化为Werner的纯态克隆结果）。类似的结论也适用于近似转置问题，两者具有相同的资源复杂度刻画。

Abstract: We consider the problem of approximate cloning of quantum states: given $n$ copies of an unknown state $ρ\in \mathbb{C}^{d \times d}$, prepare an $(n+k)$-copy state with high fidelity to $ρ^{\otimes (n+k)}$. Werner's pure state cloner is the optimal channel for the pure state case, and shows that $n = Θ(kd/\varepsilon)$ copies are necessary and sufficient to clone $k$ additional copies of an unknown pure state to fidelity $1-\varepsilon$. The random purification channel gives a straightforward extension of Werner's cloner to mixed state inputs: given $n$ copies of a mixed state, randomly purify your input, apply Werner's channel in the larger Hilbert space, and then trace out the auxiliary registers. This gives a mixed state cloner using $n = O(krd/\varepsilon)$ copies to clone rank-$r$ states. Can one do any better? We show that the answer is no: one must use $n = Ω(krd/\varepsilon)$ copies. We prove our lower bound by studying the special case of projector cloning, in which the input state $ρ$ is promised to be of the form $P/r$, where $P$ is a rank-$r$ orthogonal projector.
  As a further application of our techniques, we consider the closely related problem of approximate transposition of quantum states, where one seeks to convert $ρ^{\otimes n}$ to a $k$-copy state with high fidelity to $(ρ^T)^{\otimes k}$. Here, we again show $n = Θ(krd/\varepsilon)$ copies are necessary and sufficient for this task.

</details>

Method: 采用完全纠缠的ZZFeatureMap将每个事件编码为六量子比特态，其保真度定义量子核矩阵并嵌入标准SVM框架；将态制备与核构建解耦，使用基于MPI的多节点HPC分配分布式计算核矩阵；与线性、多项式、RBF、sigmoid SVM及极端随机树（ERT）进行基准对比；使用FakeMumbaiV2校准硬件噪声模型评估噪声影响；通过几何分析研究量子嵌入的类间/类内结构。

Result: 理想QSVC达到99.99%的最高召回率，核矩阵规模为10^5训练/4×10^5测试事件，超过先前同类研究一个数量级以上；噪声模型下AUC从0.9985降至0.9671，峰显著性从17.5降至约3.5，召回率保持99.51%；噪声使几何结构压缩向最大混合态但保留相对有序性。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了在高能物理相关规模下可扩展、可复现的量子核实验工作流，验证了量子内核在理想条件下的分类优势，同时量化了实际硬件噪声带来的性能衰减——信号保留优势虽被削弱但未完全消除，为未来量子增强分类的实用化提供了基准和代价评估。

Abstract: We present a systematic design for large-scale quantum kernel classification, demonstrated through a quantum support vector classifier (QSVC) for particle-track classification using centroid-based CLAS12 drift-chamber features. Each event is encoded into a six-qubit state via a fully entangled ZZFeatureMap, whose fidelities define a quantum kernel within a standard SVM framework. By decoupling state preparation from kernel construction and distributing evaluation across a multi-node MPI-based HPC allocation, the approach scales to 1.0x10^5 training and 4.0x10^5 test events with an exactly constructed kernel matrix, to our knowledge more than an order of magnitude larger than prior high-energy-physics quantum-kernel studies.
  Benchmarked against linear, polynomial, RBF, and sigmoid SVM kernels and extremely randomized trees (ERT), the ideal QSVC achieves the highest recall (99.99%) among all models. Under a calibrated hardware noise model (FakeMumbaiV2, 500 training / 2,000 test events), AUC falls from 0.9985 to 0.9671 and peak significance improvement falls from 17.5 to ~3.5, yet recall remains at 99.51% -- indicating this signal-retention advantage is attenuated but not eliminated by circuit-level decoherence.
  Geometric analysis of the quantum embedding shows near-orthogonal inter-class states with coherent intra-class neighborhoods under ideal simulation; under noise this structure compresses toward the maximally mixed state while preserving its relative ordering.
  These results demonstrate a scalable, reproducible workflow for quantum kernel experimentation at HEP-relevant scale, quantifying the practical cost of realistic hardware noise on quantum-enhanced classification.

</details>


### [90] [Dynamics-based nonclassicality witness under dissipative dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27337)
*Nguyen Vu Khoi Huynh,Nicky Nel Narido Labayna,Martine Schut,Valerio Scarani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于Tsirelson提出的动力学测试方法，将量子性认证协议从孤立系统扩展到含耗散的开放系统（热弛豫、纯退相干和Caldeira-Leggett模型），利用Moyal-Wigner相空间形式证明需要根据耗散强度移动经典界，并给出非经典性判据保持有效性的阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始的基于动力学的量子性测试假设系统是孤立的，但实验实现中耗散不可避免，该假设在实际中不成立。因此需要将协议扩展到更现实的含耗散环境，以确保量子性认证在实验条件下的可靠性。

Method: 采用Moyal-Wigner相空间量子力学形式，将其应用于耦合到三种标准耗散模型（热弛豫、纯退相干、Caldeira-Leggett）的谐振子系统，重新推导经典界并分析耗散对非经典性判据的影响。

Result: 发现耗散的引入需要对经典界进行耗散依赖的平移，并确定了非经典性见证保持有效性的耗散强度阈值。

Conclusion: 该工作将基于动力学的量子性认证扩展到开放量子系统，为在真实实验条件下进行量子性验证提供了理论依据。

Abstract: A dynamics-based test, originally proposed by Tsirelson for the harmonic oscillator, provides a method for certifying quantumness under the assumption of a known Hamiltonian. These tests, however, are typically proposed for isolated systems, an assumption that breaks down in experimental implementation. In this paper, we extend the protocol to the harmonic oscillator coupled to standard models of dissipation: thermal relaxation, pure dephasing, and the Caldeira--Leggett model. Using the Moyal-Wigner formalism of quantum mechanics in phase space, we show that the introduction of dissipation requires a dissipation-dependent shift of the classical bound, and provide the threshold under which the nonclassicality witness retains its validity.

</details>


### [91] [Randomness can be certified in energy-constrained semi-device-independent scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27357)
*Shashank Kumar Ranu,Lewis Wooltorton,Alastair A. Abbott,Omar Fawzi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过构造基于Navascués-Pironio-Acín(NPA)层次的半定规划松弛，证明了在能量约束的制备-测量（SDI）框架下，即使制备与测量设备之间存在预先共享的量子纠缠（完全量子敌手），仍然可以实现可认证的随机数生成，且认证下界为严格正值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有能量约束SDI框架的安全性分析假设制备和测量设备之间最多共享经典相关性。但最近研究表明，攻击者若在设备间预分发纠缠，可发动比经典相关性攻击更强大的攻击，将可提取随机数降至此前认证速率以下。这引发了根本性问题：在此场景下随机数是否还能被认证？本文旨在回答这一悬而未决的问题。

Method: 将Navascués-Pironio-Acín（NPA）层次结构适配到允许设备间共享纠缠的能量约束量子密码学设置，构造猜测概率的半定规划松弛。该方法不需要对制备与测量设备间共享的量子态维度施加任何限制，即可获得可提取随机数的认证下界。

Result: 证明在一定的能量值范围内，获得的认证随机数下界严格为正。这一结果肯定地回答了开放问题：即使在存在完全量子敌手（即设备间共享任意维度的纠缠态）的情况下，能量约束SDI框架中仍可实现可认证的随机数生成。

Conclusion: 该工作为能量约束的SDI量子密码学提供了首个在不限制共享量子态维度的条件下可证明安全的随机数认证方案，弥合了完全设备依赖与完全设备无关量子密码学之间的理论空白，表明了在更现实的量子敌手模型下随机数认证依然可行。

Abstract: The prepare-and-measure framework based on energy constraints offers a practical middle ground between fully device-dependent and device-independent quantum cryptography. The only assumption on an otherwise uncharacterized prepare-and-measure device is that the energy of the prepared states is bounded. Existing security analyses of this framework assume that the preparation and measurement devices share at most classical correlations, and under this assumption certified lower bounds on the extractable randomness have been established. Recent work has shown that an adversary who pre-distributes entanglement between the devices can mount attacks that are strictly more powerful than those available when the devices share only classical correlations, reducing the extractable randomness below the previously certified rates. This leaves open the fundamental question of whether randomness can be certified at all in this scenario.
  We address this open question by constructing semidefinite programming relaxations of the guessing probability by adapting the Navascués-Pironio-Acín hierarchy to the energy-constrained setting where shared entanglement between the devices is permitted. Our relaxations yield certified lower bounds on the extractable randomness without enforcing any restrictions on the dimensions of the quantum state shared between the preparation and measurement devices. We show that these certified lower bounds are strictly positive for a range of energy values, thereby answering the open question affirmatively: certified randomness generation is theoretically possible in the energy-constrained SDI framework even in the presence of a fully quantum adversary.

</details>


### [92] [Factorized Boolean representations for efficient quantum synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27430)
*Mehul Shah,Robert Fiszer,Marek Perkowski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明最小化的布尔表达式仍保留着逻辑最小化无法触及的代数结构（由项间的包含关系和互补极性关系产生），提取这种结构可生成操作数更多但执行成本更低的可逆电路。关键指标不是操作数，而是最宽操作的控制数（超线性成本）；提取共享因子可用少量宽操作换大量窄操作，并减少量子比特数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算需要在纠错硬件上将布尔规范翻译为可逆电路，翻译所需资源决定了可执行性。既有方法先最小化布尔表达式再映射为电路，假设最小化形式就是最优的。本文挑战该假设，指出存在逻辑最小化无法触及的代数结构资源。

Method: 从布尔表达式的项间提取包含关系和互补极性关系所隐含的代数结构；通过提取共享因子，将少量宽操作（高控制数）替换为大量窄操作（低控制数），从而降低最宽操作的控制数这一决定性成本；在表示层面证明该变换不会增加任一成本度量，再翻译为可执行电路并解除辅助线。

Result: 在量子搜索和因式分解算法的基准与预言机上，表示层面的变换从不增加任一成本度量；翻译为可执行电路后虽因须撤销辅助线而损失部分优势，但因子化电路仍使领先的电路级优化器达到比无辅助情况下更低的最终计数，且速度更快。

Conclusion: 计算的表示本身就是一种独立资源，可在编译前优化，既不同于逻辑最小化，也不同于电路级优化。

Abstract: Quantum algorithms promise advantages beyond classical reach, but running them on error-corrected hardware requires translating Boolean specifications into reversible circuits, and the resources that translation demands determine what is executable. Established methods minimize a Boolean expression and map it to a circuit, assuming the minimized form is best. Here we show that minimized expressions retain algebraic structure minimization cannot reach, arising from containment and complementary-polarity relationships among their terms, and that extracting it yields circuits cheaper to execute despite having more operations. The decisive quantity is not a circuit's operation count but the control count of its widest operation, a superlinear cost; extracting shared factors trades a few wide operations for many narrow ones and reduces qubit count. Across benchmarks and oracles from quantum search and factoring algorithms, at the representation level the transformation never increases either cost measure, a guarantee from its construction. Translation to an executable circuit returns part of that advantage, since auxiliary lines must be uncomputed, yet the factorized circuit still left a leading circuit-level optimizer reaching lower final counts, and faster, than unaided. The representation of a computation is therefore itself a resource, optimizable before compilation and distinct from both logic minimization and circuit-level optimization.

</details>

*Jingheng Zhou*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文对有限维半单代数 \Bbbk^{⊕n} 上所有（弱）双代数结构进行了系统性分类，证明双代数结构与有限幺半群一一对应，弱双代数结构则唯一决定一个有限小范畴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱双代数是双代数的自然推广，在量子群、张量范畴和表示论中具有基础性作用。作者希望通过在 \Bbbk^{⊕n} 这一具有原始正交幂等元特征基的有限维半单代数上，系统分类双代数与弱双代数结构，建立它们与幺半群和小范畴之间的对应关系，从而深化对弱双代数结构的理解。

Method: 选取有限维半单代数 \Bbbk^{⊕n}（配备原始正交幂等元的区分基），在此代数的结构基础上系统考察双代数与弱双代数的余乘法和余单位映射。通过构造双余乘与幺半群乘法之间的对应关系，证明一般双代数结构与有限幺半群之间存在双射；进一步放宽为弱双代数时，利用弱余乘的结构性质将其刻画为由有限小范畴决定的代数结构。

Result: 证明了\Bbbk^{⊕n}上的双代数结构与有限幺半群构成一一对应，且双代数同构与幺半群同构等价；对于弱双代数情形，证明了每个弱双代数结构唯一决定一个有限小范畴。

Conclusion: 该分类结果将双代数/弱双代数理论与幺半群/范畴论建立了清晰的桥梁，为量子群和表示论中相关结构的进一步研究提供了分类基础。

Abstract: Weak bialgebras are natural generalizations of bialgebras that play a fundamental role in quantum groups, tensor categories, and representation theory. In this paper, we systematically classify all (weak) bialgebra structures on the finite-dimensional semisimple algebra \(\Bbbk^{\oplus n}\), with a distinguished basis of primitive orthogonal idempotents. For the bialgebra case, we prove that such structures are in bijection with finite monoids, and that bialgebra isomorphisms correspond exactly to monoid isomorphisms. Extending the result to weak bialgebras, we show that every weak bialgebra structure uniquely determines a finite small category.

</details>

### [94] [A $σ$-McKay theorem for $π$-separable groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26373)
*David Cabrera-Berenguer*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了G. Navarro提出的关于π-可分群中Hall σ-子群的McKay猜想类比，同时推广了经典McKay猜想及其π-可分版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: McKay猜想是有限群表示论中的核心猜想，其π-可分版本已被研究，但Navarro提出了一个更一般的Hall σ-子群版本的猜想，本文旨在证明该猜想。

Method: 利用π-可分群的结构理论及Hall σ-子群的存在性，通过分析不可约特征标集合Irr_{σ'}(G)与Irr_{σ'}(N_G(H))之间的对应关系来完成证明。

Result: 证明了若G是π-可分的，p是素数，σ=π∪{p}，H是G的Hall σ-子群，则|Irr_{σ'}(G)|=|Irr_{σ'}(N_G(H))|成立。

Conclusion: 该结果统一并推广了经典McKay猜想及其π-可分版本，为Hall σ-子群框架下的特征标计数猜想的进一步研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We prove a Hall $σ$-subgroup analogue of the McKay conjecture for $π$-separable groups proposed by G. Navarro. This result simultaneously generalizes the classical McKay conjecture and its $π$-separable version. More precisely, if $G$ is $π$-separable, $p$ is a prime and $σ=π\cup\{p\}$, then it is known that there exists $H$ a Hall $σ$-subgroup of $G$. In this case, we prove that $|{\rm Irr}_{σ'}(G)|=|{\rm Irr}_{σ'}(\mathbf{N}_{G}(H))|$.

</details>


### [95] [Auslander-Reiten (n+2)-angles and local finiteness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26653)
*Jian He,Yu-Zhe Liu,Panyue Zhou*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文针对(Zhou提出的)关于( n+2 )-角范畴Grot端群关系的开问题，在n为偶数时给出了部分肯定回答：建立了充分条件使Auslander-Reiten( n+2 )-角生成Groth端群关系能够推断范畴局部有限，并证明该充分条件被广泛一类例子满足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Zhou证明了当n为奇数时，若Auslander-Reiten (n+2)-角生成Grothendieck群的关系统，则该( n+2 )-角范畴是局部有限的。然而n为偶数时这一结论是否成立仍为开放问题。本文旨在对偶数n情形给出部分解答，推动该问题的解决。

Method: 通过建立一个新的充分条件，证明在该条件下即使n为偶数，Auslander-Reiten (n+2)-角的关系统生成Grothendieck群关系也能推出范畴的局部有限性。随后构造或识别一类广泛的具体例子，验证该充分条件在多种情境下均被满足。

Result: 给出了偶数n情形下该蕴含关系成立的一个充分条件，并通过一类广泛的实例验证了该条件的适用性，表明结果不仅限于个别特例。

Conclusion: 本文对偶数n情形下的开放问题给出了部分肯定答复，拓展了( n+2 )-角范畴中Grothendieck群关系与局部有限性之间联系的已知结果范围。

Abstract: Let $\mathcal C$ be an $(n+2)$-angulated category. Zhou proved that, when $n$ is odd, if the Auslander-Reiten $(n+2)$-angles generate the relations for the Grothendieck group of $\mathcal C$, then $\mathcal C$ is locally finite. Whether the corresponding statement remains valid for even $n$ is still open. In this paper, we give a partial affirmative answer to this problem by establishing a sufficient condition under which the same implication holds for even $n$. We further show that our sufficient condition is satisfied by a broad class of examples, thereby demonstrating that the result extends well beyond isolated cases.

</details>

### [97] [Unitary Shimura Correspondence for Complex Classical Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26795)

Method: 利用Grothendieck群之间的代数结构，构造从SO(2n,C)（resp. Sp(2n,C)）的容许Harish-Chandra模到Spin(2n,C)（resp. Spin(2n+1,C)）的genuine表示的提升算子。通过研究特殊幂零轨道对应的特殊幺正表示在该提升下的像，显式确定提升结果，并利用幺正表示理论验证提升保持幺正性。

Result: 对任意特殊幂零轨道∨O对应的特殊幺正表示之和，显式确定了其提升。提升中出现的表示（若非零）均为复Spin群的genuine幺正表示，且提升算子在一大类幺正表示上保持幺正性。

Conclusion: 该提升算子在复Spin群的genuine表示研究中具有重要意义，它不仅提供了从经典复群到覆盖群表示迁移的精确工具，还证明了一大类幺正表示的幺正性在提升下得以保持，为复Spin群幺正表示的分类和构造奠定了基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we construct a lifting operator from the Grothendieck group of admissible Harish-Chandra modules of $G =\mathrm{SO}_{2n}(\mathbb C)$ (resp. $\mathrm{Sp}_{2n}(\mathbb C)$) to that of genuine representations of $\mathrm{Spin}_{2n}(\mathbb C)$ (resp. $\mathrm{Spin}_{2n+1}(\mathbb C)$). We determine the lift of the sum of special unipotent representations attached to any ${}^{\vee}\mathcal O \subseteq {}^{\vee}\mathfrak g$ explicitly. In particular, the representations occurring in these lifts, if nonzero, are genuine unipotent representations of complex Spin groups and are unitary. As a consequence, the lifting operator preserves unitarity on a large class of unitary representations.

</details>

Method: 通过计算所有分层和Dynkin类型下的Saito行列式，分析其整除性，证明其可被限制根之q-模拟的乘积整除，从而将Weyl分母公式的因子分解推广到更高余维分层。

Result: 成功计算了所有分层和Dynkin类型下的Saito行列式，证明了其整除性，同时推广了Weyl分母公式的因子分解，并为Antoniou-Feigin-Strachan关于Coxeter群的结果提供了q-模拟细化。

Conclusion: 本文为扩展仿射Weyl群的Saito度量建立了系统性的因子分解理论，同时统一和推广了已有的相关结果。

Abstract: We investigate the form of the Saito metric on quotients of the reflection representation of an extended affine Weyl group, restricted to an arbitrary stratum of its discriminant. We compute the Saito determinant for all strata and Dynkin types and show that it is divisible by a product of q-analogues of restricted roots for the associated hyperplane arrangement. Our results simultaneously generalise the factorisation in the Weyl denominator formula to higher codimension strata, and provide q-analogue refinements of results of Antoniou-Feigin-Strachan for Coxeter groups.

</details>


### [99] [Noncommutative Cluster Varieties and Moduli Spaces of Local Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27284)
*Zachary Greenberg,Dani Kaufman,Merik Niemeyer,Anna Wienhard*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文为每个既约根系R和带标记曲面S构造了非交换簇变体A_{R,S}，统一推广了Fock-Goncharov、Li、Goncharov-Shen等多个已有的簇变体理论；同时定义了Jordan分裂群，通过Tits-Kantor-Koecher构造与分裂李代数构造的统一来构建其李代数。文章还研究了这些群的R-分次结构、A_{R,S}的Jordan代数点与G-局部系统的参数化关系，以及非交换簇结构在R-Bruhat胞腔上的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的簇变体和非交换簇代数理论分散在多条研究路线中（Fock-Goncharov的X簇、Berenstein-Retakh的基于Jordan代数的构造、此前作者引入的多边形簇代数等），缺乏统一的框架。同时，Jordan代数与代数群之间的深刻联系（Tits-Kantor-Koecher构造）尚未在簇变体理论中得到系统化整合。作者旨在建立一个统一理论，将根系、带标记曲面、Jordan代数与代数群有机地联系起来。

Method: (1) 为每个既约根系R和带标记曲面S构造成族A_{R,S}的簇变体；(2) 定义Jordan分裂群G，通过统一Tits-Kantor-Koecher构造（单个Jordan代数）与分裂李代数构造来构建其李代数；(3) 利用标准抛物子代数p_Θ诱导R-分次，并分类R-分次对(G,Θ)；(4) 定义A_{R,S}的Jordan代数点，用于参数化G-局部系统和带边界装饰的构型；(5) 在G的R-Bruhat胞腔上给出非交换簇结构，推广Berenstein-Fomin-Zelevinsky的簇代数；(6) 在形式实Jordan代数情形下定义正结构，推广Guichard-Wienhard的正结构理论。

Result: 文章成功构建了统一框架A_{R,S}，涵盖多种已知簇变体作为特例；给出了R-分次对(G,Θ)的完整分类，其条件仅依赖于简单根子集Θ；在S为圆盘时，A_{R,S}的点参数化带装饰旗的构型，并在G的R-Bruhat胞腔上建立了非交换簇结构；在形式实Jordan代数情形下，证明了具有正结构的(G,Θ)对恰好是Guichard-Wienhard意义下可染上正结构的对，并给出了正旗构型和正表示许多性质的代数证明。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个统一的理论框架，将非交换簇变体与Jordan分裂群深度结合，同时推广了多条独立发展的研究路线。该工作不仅提供了R-分次群的完整分类，还通过正结构理论架起了代数与几何（正旗构型、正表示）之间的桥梁。

Abstract: In this article, we construct noncommutative cluster varieties, $\mathcal{A}_{R,S}$, for each reduced root system $R$ and marked surface $S$ simultaneously generalizing the cluster varieties of Fock-Goncharov, Li, Goncharov-Shen, Berenstein-Retakh, Goncharov-Kontsevich, and our previously introduced polygonal cluster algebras. Additionally, we define a large class of algebraic groups, we call Jordan split groups. Given a reduced root system $R$ and a family of Jordan algebras, the Lie algebra for $G$ is constructed by unifying the Tits-Kantor-Koecher construction for a single Jordan algebra with the construction of a split Lie algebra.
  The notion of Jordan split groups is closely related to a grading of its Lie algebra by the root system $R$. We show that these gradings are usually induced by a choice of standard parabolic subalgebra $\mathfrak{p}_Θ$ and we classify $R$-graded pairs $(G,Θ)$ via a condition depending only on the subset $Θ\subset Δ$ of the set of simple roots. Next, we define Jordan algebra points of $\mathcal{A}_{R,S}$ which parameterize $G$-local systems on $S$ with boundary decoration related to cosets $G/U_Θ$ when $G$ is Jordan split of type $R$. When $S$ is a disk, points of $\mathcal{A}_{R,S}$ parameterize configurations of decorated flags. We use this to give noncommutative cluster structures on the double $R$-Bruhat cells of $G$, generalizing the cluster algebras of Berenstein-Fomin-Zelevinsky. When each Jordan algebra is formally real, we say that $G$ has a positive structure with respect to $Θ$. This defines a positive semigroup in $G$. For real algebraic groups, the pairs $(G,Θ)$ which have positive structures are exactly those which admit a positive structure as defined by Guichard-Wienhard and we give algebraic proofs of many of the properties of positive configurations of flags and of positive representations.

</details>
