### [134] [The facts on quantum self-dual Einstein gravity and self-dual Yang-Mills theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26242)
*Noah Miller*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文是对四维自对偶爱因斯坦引力(SDG)和自对偶杨-米尔斯理论(SDYM)的教学性综述，系统阐述了SDG作为一回路精确、紫外和红外有限、微分同胚不变但非酉的量子引力理论的核心性质，并全面回顾了从作用量推导、散射振幅计算到天体全息相关结果的一系列关键技术和方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自对偶爱因斯坦引力(SDG)是四维量子引力的重要玩具模型，具有一回路精确且UV/IR有限的独特性质，其非平庸散射振幅（一回路全正螺旋度振幅）与纯爱因斯坦引力完全一致，为研究量子引力提供了难得的精确计算平台。作者希望通过教学性综述，系统梳理SDG和SDYM领域的关键计算技术，填补文献中的空白，并为后续研究指明方向。

Method: 本文采用综述性、教学性方法，涵盖以下关键技术：(1) 从完整爱因斯坦引力/杨-米尔斯作用量在光锥规范下推导自对偶作用量；(2) 利用Berends-Giele流计算散射振幅；(3) 证明树级一负螺旋度振幅的消失；(4) 通过双重离壳流计算一回路全正螺旋度振幅；(5) 使用维数平移和幺正切割技术；(6) 采用BDPR引力公式处理引力振幅；(7) 讨论天体Lw_{1+\infty}对称性、微扰子(Perturbiners)等结构，以及自对偶理论的协变表述与规范固定。

Result: 文章系统给出了SDG和SDYM的一回路全正螺旋度振幅的精确计算结果，确认了SDG的一回路精确性（即无更高回路修正），阐明了鬼场解耦机制，建立了自对偶理论振幅与纯爱因斯坦引力振幅的等价关系，并回顾了天体全息程序中与SDG相关的选定结果，同时提出了一个n点一回路一负螺旋度引力振幅的全n表达式。

Conclusion: SDG和SDYM为理解量子引力中的精确可计算性提供了重要的理论框架。SDG作为一回路精确的非酉量子引力理论，其振幅计算技术（Berends-Giele流、双重离壳流、维数平移等）为更广泛的研究提供了方法论基础。作者指出的未来研究方向包括：进一步探索一回路一负螺旋度振幅的全n表达式、深化SDG与天体全息中Lw_{1+\infty}对称性的联系，以及完善自对偶理论的协变表述。

Abstract: Self-dual Einstein gravity is a one-loop exact, UV and IR finite, diffeomorphism invariant, non-unitary theory of quantum gravity in four dimensions. The only non-trivial amplitudes are the one-loop-all-plus amplitudes, and they are equal to those of pure Einstein gravity.
  In these notes we review SDG and SDYM pedagogically. We walk the reader through key calculations, fill in some gaps in the literature, and point out directions for future research. Topics include: derivation of the self-dual actions from the full Einstein gravity/YM actions in lightcone gauge, decoupling of ghosts, Berends-Giele currents, vanishing of tree-level-one-minus amplitudes, celestial $Lw_{1+\infty}$ symmetry, perturbiners, double-off-shell current calculation of one-loop-all-plus SDYM and SDG amplitudes, dimension shifting, unitarity cuts, the BDPR gravity formula, one-loop color ordering, and the covariant formulations of the self-dual theories as well as their their gauge fixing. We also review some selected results from the celestial holography program, and briefly comment on an all-n expression for the one-loop-one-minus gravity amplitudes.

</details>


### [135] [Renormalization in Liouville gravity and stochastic inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26244)
*Jordan Cotler,Victor Ivo,Juan Maldacena*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了由具有对数相关性的随机高斯场ζ(x)定义的d维随机几何（度规ds²=e^{2ζ}dx²），探讨了体积元e^{dζ}的重整化问题，证明了KPZ标度关系可以从物理截断到固定微扰截断的粗粒化过程中直接推导出来，并揭示了Liouville体积重整化在涨落超过临界值时失效对应于永恒膨胀的转变，在d=2时对应已知的c_m=1壁垒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受通胀理论和Liouville理论的启发，作者希望统一理解随机几何中体积元的重整化问题。这两个领域分别独立发展了体积元重整化的公式（KPZ公式和通胀公式），但缺乏统一的物理图像。理解这一联系有助于揭示随机几何中的临界行为，并可能将永恒膨胀的转变与Liouville引力中的已知壁垒（c_m=1）联系起来。

Method: 文章从固定的物理截断出发，通过将算符粗粒化到由平坦度规dx²定义的固定参考截断，直接物理推导了KPZ标度关系。将同一个重整化问题映射到随机通胀框架中，将波动场类比为暴胀子场的布朗运动，分析体积元重整化的失效条件与永恒膨胀转变之间的对应关系。

Result: 给出了KPZ标度关系的直接物理推导；证明了Liouville体积元重整化的失效临界点对应于永恒膨胀的转变；在d=2时该临界点精确对应于Liouville引力中已知的c_m=1壁垒；建立了与随机表面数学概率方法（如GFF、对数相关高斯场）的联系。

Conclusion: 通胀理论和Liouville理论中的体积元重整化本质上是同一物理问题，其临界行为给出了永恒膨胀的边界条件，并在二维情形精确对应Liouville引力的中心荷临界值，这为随机几何的物理理解和数学方法之间架起了桥梁。

Abstract: Motivated by inflation and Liouville theory, we consider random $d$-dimensional geometries characterized by an overall scale factor $ds^2 = e^{2 ζ} dx^2 $ given in terms of a random Gaussian field $ζ(x)$ with logarithmic correlations. We discuss aspects of the renormalization of the volume element $e^{d ζ}$, connecting well-known Liouville theory formulas (KPZ) and inflationary ones. By starting from a fixed physical cutoff and coarse-graining operators to a fixed fiducial cutoff, defined via the flat metric $dx^2$, we provide a direct physical derivation of the KPZ scaling relation. We point out that the same renormalization problem arises in stochastic inflation, where it is modeled by Brownian motion of the inflaton field. We show that the breakdown of the renormalization of the Liouville volume when the fluctuation amplitude exceeds a critical value corresponds to the transition to eternal inflation. In $d = 2$, this matches the familiar $c_m = 1$ barrier in Liouville gravity. We also discuss connections to mathematical probabilistic approaches to random surfaces.

</details>


### [136] [Positivity in energy correlators and the event distribution formula](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26246)
*Alexandre Belin,Nathan Borak,Johan Henriksson,Romain Piron,Alexander Zhiboedov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文论证了能量关联子无限层级正性问题的普遍解由事件分布公式给出，并推导出二点和三点能量关联子多极展开系数的双侧界，这些界比单独要求正性得到的界更强且最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能量关联子作为广泛理论（规范理论、共形场论、弦论）中的普适可观测量，受正性约束，但其无限层级正性问题的完整解尚不清楚，需要建立严格的数学框架来刻画其约束结构。

Method: 通过事件分布公式将能量关联子表达为天体球面上概率测度空间的矩，利用正性条件（点态正性、全局正性、能量守恒）推导无限层级的正性约束，并具体分析二点与三点能量关联子的多极展开系数界。

Result: 证明了无限层级正性约束比单独二点、三点正性强，得到的低自旋多极系数界是最优的（极值可由有限粒子事件的有限混合实现），并在对撞机物理和共形场论中展示了正性后果。

Conclusion: 能量关联子的无限层级正性完全由事件分布公式刻画，其蕴含的双侧界是最优且非平凡的，对唯象学和共形场论研究具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Energy correlators are universal observables, well defined across a wide range of theories and spacetime dimensions, from gauge theory and conformal field theory to string theory. Energy correlators are constrained by three fundamental properties: pointwise positivity of the energy flux, global positivity originating from their interpretation as state norms in a unitary theory, and energy conservation organizing multi-point energy correlators into an infinite consistent hierarchy. We argue that the most general solution to the infinite hierarchy positivity problem of energy correlators is given by the event distribution formula, which expresses energy correlators as moments of the measure on the space of probability measures on the celestial sphere. We work out in detail implications of positivity for two- and three-point energy correlators and show that it implies nontrivial two-sided bounds on their multipole expansion coefficients. The bounds obtained by requiring consistency of the infinite hierarchy of energy correlators are strictly stronger than those obtained by imposing positivity of the two- and three-point correlators alone. For the low-spin multipole coefficients studied in the paper, the derived bounds are optimal in the sense that their extrema are realized by finite mixtures of finite-particle events. We further demonstrate consequences of positivity in energy correlators in collider physics and conformal field theories.

</details>


### [137] [Why Cooper pairs live in AdS2: a spectral analysis of the Yukawa-SYK model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26251)
*Veronika Carolin Stangier,Jörg Schmalian*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文建立了Yukawa-SYK模型中Cooper配对的几何表述的谱学基础。作者从大N双局域有效作用量出发，推导了偶频自旋单态Cooper通道的高斯涨落核，并按照Maldacena和Stanford的构造，解析地确定了任意共形权重h下的核本征值k(h)，将配对涨落分解为dS₂空间拉普拉斯算子的连续和离散谱部分。结果表明超导不稳定性和其附近普适低能涨落完全存在于连续散射谱中，而离散模非临界。将核在最低连续模附近展开得到dS₂上的Klein-Gordon作用量（限制在连续谱子空间），这正是逆Radon变换到AdS₂有明确定义的子空间。因此投影后的双局域Cooper对场可映射为AdS₂中传播的标量物质场，无需对体理论做进一步谱限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Yukawa-SYK模型中，Cooper配对的几何表述（即全息对偶表述）此前隐含了某个投影假设，但缺乏微观理论层面的严格推导和谱学基础验证。本文旨在填补这一空白：通过精确分析配对涨落核的谱结构，阐明为何以及在何种谱子空间上可以将双局域配对理论映射到AdS₂中的局部体场，从而为几何化/全息化表述提供微观依据。

Method: 本文采用解析方法：从Yukawa-SYK模型的大N双局域有效作用量出发，构造高斯涨落核；随后遵循Maldacena和Stanford的求解框架，解析求解核本征值k(h)对任意共形权重h的依赖；通过将涨落核与dS₂空间上的拉普拉斯算子相关联，将谱分解为连续散射部分和离散部分；在最低连续模附近对核进行展开，得到限制在连续谱子空间的Klein-Gordon作用量，并利用逆Radon变换实现到AdS₂的映射。

Result: 主要结果：(1) 解析确定了任意共形权重h下Cooper通道涨落核的本征值k(h)；(2) 证明超导不稳定性和附近普适低能涨落完全位于连续散射谱，离散模非临界；(3) 在最低连续模附近展开核得到dS₂上限制于连续谱子空间的Klein-Gordon作用量；(4) 证明该连续谱子空间恰好是逆Radon变换到AdS₂有明确定义的子空间，因此双局域Cooper对场可完整映射为AdS₂标量场，无需额外谱限制。

Conclusion: 本文为Yukawa-SYK模型中Cooper配对的几何表述提供了缺失的微观谱学正当性证明，阐明了局部体场是如何从双局域配对理论中涌现的。该工作澄清了早期全息表述中隐含投影的物理本质，即对连续散射谱子空间的自然限制，并建立了从微观配对理论到AdS₂体理论的明确对应关系。

Abstract: We establish the spectral foundation of the geometric formulation of Cooper pairing in the Yukawa--Sachdev--Ye--Kitaev model. Starting from the large-$N$ bilocal effective action, we derive the Gaussian fluctuation kernel in the even-frequency spin-singlet Cooper channel. Following the construction of Maldacena and Stanford, we determine the kernel eigenvalue $k\left(h\right)$ analytically for arbitrary conformal weight $h$ and resolve the pairing fluctuations into the continuous and discrete sectors of the associated de-Sitter space Laplacian. We show that the superconducting instability and the universal low-energy fluctuations near it reside entirely in the continuous scattering sector, while the discrete modes remain non-critical. Expanding the kernel about the lowest continuum mode yields a Klein--Gordon action on ${\rm dS}_2$, restricted to the continuous spectral subspace. This is precisely the subspace on which the inverse Radon transform to ${\rm AdS}_2$ is well defined. The projected bilocal Cooper-pair field can therefore be mapped onto a scalar matter field propagating in ${\rm AdS}_2$, without requiring any further spectral restriction on the bulk theory. Our results provide the missing microscopic justification for the projection implicit in earlier holographic formulations and clarify how a local bulk field emerges from the bilocal pairing theory.

</details>


### [138] [Quantum Chaos and Spread of States in Krylov Subspace: A Topical Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26825)
*Hyun-Sik Jeong,Juan F. Pedraza*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文综述了Krylov态复杂性（又称扩散复杂性）作为量子混沌、信息传播和多体动力学诊断工具的最新进展，涵盖其理论基础（Lanczos算法和最优基定理）、混沌系统中的过冲特征、随机矩阵普适类联系，以及全息描述和未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子混沌的定量诊断一直是量子多体物理的核心问题。Krylov复杂性提供了一个基于Lanczos算法和最优基定理的稳健谱学窗口，能够从光谱统计的角度区分混沌与可积系统，并连接随机矩阵理论、量子引力全息原理等不同领域，因此需要系统性地综述这一快速发展的方向。

Method: 本文采用综述性方法，从Krylov复杂性的数学构造（Lanczos算法、最优基定理与Krylov链）出发，系统梳理其在多种模型中的应用，包括量子台球、量子自旋链和SYK模型变体，并讨论其在爱因斯坦引力中的全息对应关系。同时提供Mathematica笔记本以支持数值探索。

Result: 综述指出混沌系统的标志性特征是复杂性在演化过程中的过冲（overshoot）现象——混沌演化将状态推入Krylov链更深处，超过可积系统后再弛豫至平衡。该行为与随机矩阵普适类中的谱统计紧密相关，并在多种模型中普遍成立。

Conclusion: Krylov复杂性已确立为量子混沌和动力学中一种通用且锐利的诊断工具，但仍有若干开放问题，包括时间依赖系统的推广、量子场论形式的构建以及更完整的全息描述，这些构成了未来研究的重要方向。

Abstract: Krylov state complexity, or spread complexity, has emerged as a sharp and versatile diagnostic of quantum chaos, information spreading, and many-body dynamics. Built from the Lanczos algorithm and grounded in the optimal-basis theorem, Krylov complexity thereby provides a robust spectroscopic window into quantum dynamics. A central theme is the characteristic overshoot observed in chaotic systems: a complexity peak in which chaotic evolution drives the state deeper into the Krylov chain than in integrable systems before relaxing to equilibrium. This behavior, tied to random-matrix universality classes of spectral statistics, is illustrated across a broad range of models, including quantum billiards, quantum spin chains, and variants of the SYK model. We also discuss proposed holographic descriptions of Krylov complexity in Einstein gravity, and conclude by outlining future directions and open problems, including time-dependent systems and quantum-field-theoretic formulations. A Mathematica notebook is provided for numerical exploration of Krylov complexity and spectral statistics across models.

</details>


### [139] [Inner products and closed universes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26253)
*Jordan Cotler,Kristan Jensen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了半经典德西特量子引力中晚期态的重叠。在de Sitter JT引力的变形族中，计算了无边界波函数到单圈阶并推导出超局域晚期测度。单宇宙对范数的贡献恰好是球面振幅的八倍，因此洛伦兹测度所用的配对与欧几里得引力中隐含的配对不同。在对分离的最终宇宙求和后，无边界范数指数化为单宇宙结果，给出⟨HH|HH⟩≈exp(8Z_sphere)而非闭几何求和Z_closed≈exp(Z_sphere)。文章还回顾了正宇宙学常数Einstein引力中的类似失配，并讨论了微扰引力中切割与粘合的含义以及扩展到拓扑求和时的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解半经典德西特量子引力中晚期量子态的归一化与内积结构。核心动机在于厘清洛伦兹测度中隐含的态配对与欧几里得引力路径积分中隐含的配对之间的差异，并探讨这对微扰引力中切割-粘合规则以及拓扑求和的影响。

Method: 在de Sitter JT引力的一族变形中，作者计算了无边界波函数到单圈阶，推导出超局域晚期测度，并通过单宇宙范数贡献（精确等于球面振幅的八倍）指数化求和得到多宇宙总范数。同时类比分析了正宇宙学常数Einstein引力中晚期无边界范数在单圈阶消失的情况，与欧几里得球面振幅形成对比。

Result: 主要发现包括：(1) 单宇宙对范数的贡献恰好是球面振幅的8倍；(2) 无边界范数指数化了单宇宙结果，得到⟨HH|HH⟩≈exp(8Z_sphere)，而非闭几何求和给出的Z_closed≈exp(Z_sphere)；(3) 在Einstein引力中，晚期无边界范数在单圈阶消失，与欧几里得球面振幅不同，表明存在系统性失配。

Conclusion: 晚期态重叠加法出洛伦兹测度与欧几里得引力中隐含的配对存在本质差异（8倍因子），说明量子引力中切割-粘合以及态空间的构建需要更精细的处理。作者讨论了这些结果对微扰引力中切割-粘合规则的含义，以及当构造推广到拓扑求和时会涌现的新特征。

Abstract: We study overlaps of late-time states in semiclassical de Sitter quantum gravity. In a family of deformations of de Sitter JT gravity, we compute the no-boundary wavefunction to one-loop and derive the ultralocal late-time measure. The one-universe contribution to the norm is exactly eight times the sphere amplitude, so the pairing used by the Lorentzian measure differs from that implicit in Euclidean gravity. After summing over disconnected final universes, the no-boundary norm exponentiates the one-universe result, giving $\langle \!\langle \text{HH}|\text{HH}\rangle\!\rangle \approx \exp(8Z_{\rm sphere})$ rather than the sum over closed geometries, $Z_{\rm closed}\approx \exp(Z_{\rm sphere})$. We also review an analogous mismatch in Einstein gravity with positive cosmological constant, where the late-time no-boundary norm vanishes at one-loop, differing from the Euclidean sphere amplitude. We discuss the implications for cutting and gluing in perturbative gravity, and features that arise when extending this construction to sums over topologies.

</details>


### [140] [Contact Structure-induced Symplectic Nearly Half-Flat $SU(3)$ Structure in Finite-$N$ Thermal ${\cal M}$-Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26267)
*Aalok Misra*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明了与有限温度QCD类理论对偶的M-理论（MQGP构型）中，由G₂结构七流形上的接触三结构所诱导的横向SU(3)-结构属于辛近半平坦（S(nearly)HF）类，并利用该G-结构分类首次系统给出Kaluza-Klein约化产生的径向磁场，其满足Bessel型ODE，与IIA型弦理论中D6-膜磁场通过非线性幂律关联，为有限温度弦理论中的弱磁场生成提供了全息机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作已构造了MQGP（M-理论QGP）对偶，识别出其七流形M₇支持接触三结构并诱导横向SU(3)-结构，但该诱导结构的具体Chiossi-Salamon挠率类尚未确定，导致G-结构分类不完整。此外，缺乏该结构在物理可观测物理量（如磁场）上的系统应用。

Method: 作者首先分析C3S诱导的横向SU(3)-结构的闭合条件与挠率张量，证明dω_Φ=0精确成立且dΩ_+在MQGP极限下被指数压制，从而将其归类为S(nearly)HF，唯一非零挠率类为W₂⁻。随后对G₂-结构正三形式微分的霍奇对偶进行KK约化，利用G₂/S(nearly)HF挠率数据组织约化过程，得到径向磁场B_KK(r)；通过Bessel型ODE的线性分析（规则奇点在视界处），结合可归一化与红外正则性条件选择J₂解，并分析内部因子的调和性及大N压制。

Result: (1) 严格证明了MQGP对偶中横向SU(3)-结构属于S(nearly)HF类，dω_Φ=0精确成立，dΩ_+为O(e^{-N^{1/3}/O(1)})量级的指数抑制，唯一非零Chiossi-Salamon挠率类为W₂⁻，完成了G-结构分类。(2) 首次系统性得到KK约化产生的径向磁场B_KK(r)，其平方范数精确分解为外因子与内因子之积。(3) B_KK满足具有规则奇点的Bessel型ODE，可归一化与红外正则性唯一选定J₂解。(4) B_KK与IIA味D6-膜的磁场之间满足非线性幂律关系，为有限温度弦理论中的弱磁场生成提供了自上而下的全息机制。

Conclusion: 本文完成了MQGP对偶的G-结构挠率分类（S(nearly)HF），并揭示了该几何结构在磁场物理中的直接应用：KK约化磁场满足可归一化的Bessel型方程，并通过非线性幂律与D6-膜磁场关联，为全息QCD框架下有限温度弱磁场生成提供了自洽的自上而下描述。

Abstract: The ${\cal M}$-theory dual of thermal QCD-like theories constructed in arXiv:1306.4339, 2004.07259 involves, as shown in arXiv:2211.13186, a closed $G_2$-structure seven-fold $\mathcal{M}_7$ supporting Contact 3-Structures (C3S) that induce a ``transverse'' $SU(3)$-structure. We prove that this induced structure belongs to the symplectic nearly half-flat (S(nearly)HF) class: $dω_Φ=0$ exactly, while $dΩ_+$ is suppressed as $\mathcal{O}(e^{-N^{1/3}/\mathcal{O}(1)})$ in the infrared "MQGP limit", leaving $W_Φ^{SU(3)}=W_2^-$ as the sole non-vanishing Chiossi--Salamon torsion class and completing the $G$-structure classification of the MQGP dual initiated in arXiv:1507.02692,2004.07259, 2211.13186. As a physical application, Kaluza--Klein reduction of the eleven-dimensional Hodge dual of the differential of the $G_2$-structure positive three-form on $\mathcal{M}_7$, organised through the $G_2$/S(nearly)HF torsion data, yields a radial ``magnetic field'' $\mathcal{B}_{\rm KK}(r)$ whose squared norm factorises exactly into external and internal factors. $\mathcal{B}_{\rm KK}(r)$ satisfies a Bessel-type ODE with a regular singular point at the horizon; normalisability and infrared regularity select $J_2$, with harmonicity of the aforementioned internal factor recovered from the large-$N$ suppression. $\mathcal{B}_{\rm KK}$ is related to the magnetic field of the type~IIA flavour $D6$-branes via a non-linear power law, furnishing a top-down holographic mechanism for generating weak magnetic fields in finite-temperature string theory.

</details>


### [141] [Gravitational Compton Amplitude to All Orders in Perturbation Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26284)
*Miguel Correia,Giulia Isabella,Anna M. Wolz*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文展示了在世界线有效场论框架下，如何高效计算引力波在紧致天体上散射的振幅至牛顿常数G的任意阶。该方法通过求解分波振幅的有效波动方程（Wilson系数即潮汐Love数通过短距离边界条件进入），然后将分波求和得到用椭圆多对数表示的动量空间Compton振幅。作者重现了O(G^4)的近期结果，并获得O(G^7)的新预测，其中Love数首次在O(G^5)贡献。更重要的是，作者在O(G^7)发现了经典引力振幅中的首个紫外发散，表明纯点粒子描述在广义相对论中不一致；通过与黑洞微扰理论匹配，证明静态Love数在壳上消失，并预测了次领头阶非零的Schwarzschild黑洞Love数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在引力波天文学时代，精确计算引力波散射振幅对于理解双体动力学和检验广义相对论至关重要。传统方法在计算高阶G阶数时计算量庞大，且黑洞Love数（表征潮汐形变的响应函数）的性质也存在争议——静态黑洞的Love数通常被认为为零，但其高阶贡献尚不清楚。作者旨在建立一种能够高效、系统地计算到任意G阶的方法，并澄清黑洞Love数的性质。

Method: 作者采用世界线有效场论(worldline EFT)框架，将紧致天体视为有效世界线。关键方法创新是：构造一个分波振幅满足的有效波动方程，其中Wilson系数（即Love数）通过短距离边界条件进入；通过求解该方程获得各阶G的分波振幅，再进行分波求和（partial-wave summation），将结果用椭圆多对数（elliptic polylogarithms）表示；最后与黑洞微扰理论（black hole perturbation theory, BHPT）匹配以确定匹配系数和Love数。

Result: (1) 重现了O(G^4)的已知结果，并将计算推进到O(G^7)；(2) Love数首次在O(G^5)阶出现贡献；(3) 在O(G^7)阶发现了经典引力振幅中的首个紫外发散，证明纯点粒子描述在广义相对论中不自洽；(4) 静态Love数在壳上（on-shell）为零，但预测了次领头阶非零的Schwarzschild黑洞Love数。

Conclusion: 该工作为高效计算引力波散射振幅提供了一种系统性的新方法，大幅扩展了可计算的G阶数。O(G^7)处的紫外发散是一个重要发现，表明需要引入新的物理（如黑洞的内部结构或量子引力效应）来完善理论描述。此外，静态Love数在壳上为零与次领头阶非零Love数的发现，深化了对黑洞潮汐响应性质的理解，对引力波天文学中波形模板的构建具有直接意义。

Abstract: We show how amplitudes from the scattering of gravitational waves off compact objects in worldline effective field theory can be efficiently computed to arbitrary order in Newton's constant $G$. Our approach solves an effective wave equation for the partial-wave amplitude in which Wilson coefficients (tidal Love numbers) enter through boundary conditions at short distances. We then perform the sum over partial waves to obtain the momentum-space Compton amplitude in terms of elliptic polylogarithms. We reproduce recent results through $\mathcal{O}(G^4)$ and obtain new predictions up to $\mathcal{O}(G^7)$, with Love numbers first contributing at $\mathcal{O}(G^5)$. We find the first ultraviolet divergence in a classical gravitational amplitude at $\mathcal{O}(G^7)$, showing that a pure point-particle description is not consistent in general relativity. Matching to black hole perturbation theory, we show that static Love numbers vanish on-shell and predict subleading non-zero Schwarzschild black hole Love numbers.

</details>


### [142] [Vortex-Antivortex Annihilation from BPS Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26333)
*J. P. S. Neto,J. G. F. Campos,A. Mohammadi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文引入了一个涡旋-杂质模型，允许BPS构型同时包含涡旋和反涡旋，并允许在模空间近似下描述涡旋-反涡旋湮灭过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BPS涡旋模型通常只允许同号涡旋共存，难以在模空间框架内描述涡旋与反涡旋的相互作用及湮灭过程。本文旨在通过引入特定类型的杂质来突破这一限制，使得涡旋-反涡旋湮灭可在模空间近似中得到描述。

Method: 引入涡旋-杂质模型，通过坐标变换将含杂质的BPS方程映射到无杂质的Abelian-Higgs模型；识别非局域化杂质作为"镜像"产生对称放置的涡旋-反涡旋对；推导对应的模空间度量；将构造推广到交替涡旋-反涡旋链和二维阵列；引入径向凸起杂质产生具有非平凡内部磁结构的拉伸和压缩涡旋。

Result: 成功构造了包含涡旋和反涡旋的BPS构型，证明在测地线近似下涡旋-反涡旋对渐近接近湮灭构型；获得了相应的模空间度量；将方法推广至链和二维阵列结构；通过径向凸起杂质实现了具有非平凡内部磁结构的拉伸/压缩涡旋。

Conclusion: 该工作为在BPS框架下研究涡旋-反涡旋湮灭提供了新的理论工具，拓展了模空间近似的适用范围，并为进一步研究涡旋动力学（包括湮灭过程）奠定了基础。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce a vortex-impurity model that admits BPS configurations containing both vortices and antivortices and allows vortex-antivortex annihilation to be described within the moduli-space approximation. For a suitable class of impurities, the BPS equations can be mapped through a coordinate transformation onto those of the impurity-free Abelian-Higgs model. In particular, we identify nonlocalized impurities that act as mirrors, generating a vortex-antivortex pair symmetrically placed with respect to the impurity. We derive the corresponding moduli-space metric and show that, within the geodesic approximation, the pair approaches the annihilation configuration asymptotically. We further extend the construction to chains and two-dimensional arrays of alternating vortices and antivortices. Finally, we introduce radial bump impurities that produce stretched and compressed vortices with nontrivial internal magnetic structures.

</details>


### [143] [The Capacity of Entanglement and Holographic Entropies at Finite Resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26377)
*Ning Bao,Jacob March*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新审视Ryu-Takayanagi公式的两个未决问题：(1)用纠缠容量替代Fannes-Audenaert维度界，给出无维度依赖且可饱和的熵不确定性上界；(2)论证当单个replica saddle在α=1附近主导时，光滑Rényi熵及单次(min/max)熵均与S_vN一致到O(√S_vN)，从而证明最小表面同时固定所有单次熵，大中心荷扮演了渐近等分性质中大副本数的角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ryu-Takayanagi公式将最小表面面积与边界子区域的von Neumann熵等同，但留下两个开放问题：一是几何如何尖锐地固定熵（Fannes-Audenaert界发散的维度依赖问题），二是面积如何定义单个状态下的熵（因为压缩/稀释率只对多副本定义而几何只描述单副本）。

Method: 作者引入纠缠容量（entanglement capacity，即模能量的方差）替代维度因子，证明其平方根与纠缠面积的平方根同阶增长，而非维度因子随正则体积发散。对于单副本问题，通过分析α=1附近单个replica saddle的主导性，推导光滑Rényi熵和内/外熵与S_vN的一致性到O(√S_vN)阶。

Result: 给出无维度依赖且可饱和的熵不确定性界，使熵的模糊性对任何容量远小于S_vN²·trace norm的扰动都是次广延的；证明了在单replica saddle主导下，所有光滑Rényi熵与平滑min/max熵均与S_vN一致到O(√S_vN)，并以大中心荷替代大副本数。作为推论，限定了全息态偏离全息熵锥的程度。

Conclusion: 最小表面不仅固定von Neumann熵，还同时固定所有单次熵量；大中心荷在全息设置中起到了与渐近等分性质中副本数相同的角色。熵锥偏离被约束，但估计与认证问题仍留作开放问题。

Abstract: The Ryu-Takayanagi formula equates the area of a minimal surface with the von Neumann entropy of a boundary subregion, and leaves two things about that identification open.
  The first is how sharply a geometry fixes an entropy. Fannes-Audenaert answers with a Hilbert-space dimension, which diverges as the cutoff is removed however close the two states are. We replace it with the capacity of entanglement, the variance of the modular energy, whose square root grows like the square root of the entangling area where the dimensional factor grows like the regulated volume. The bound is dimension-free and saturated, and it makes the ambiguity of the entropy subextensive for any perturbation whose capacity is small compared with $S_{vN}^2$ times the trace norm.
  The second is what the area means for a single state, since compression and dilution rates are defined only for many copies while a geometry describes one. When a single replica saddle dominates near $α= 1$, every smooth Rényi entropy at fixed $α> 1$ agrees with $S_{vN}$ to $\textit{O}(\sqrt{S_{vN}})$, as do the smooth min- and max-entropies. The minimal surface therefore fixes every one-shot entropy of the state at once, with large central charge playing the role of large copy number in the asymptotic equipartition property.
  As a consequence we bound how far outside the holographic entropy cone a holographic state can appear to fall, leaving estimation and certification open.

</details>


### [144] [Scalar QED effective action in dS: implications for primordial magnetogenesis and the running of coupling constants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26443)
*Ehsan Bavarsad,Sang Pyo Kim,Clément Stahl,She-Sheng Xue*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文在de Sitter时空中，通过gamma函数正则化的in-out方法，精确推导了带电标量场在均匀电场和平行磁场下的单圈有效作用量，发现真空极化会导致磁场获得非零真空期望值，可能解释早期宇宙的原初磁生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同时存在电磁场和de Sitter时空背景时，粒子产生与真空极化问题可以非微扰地探测量子电动力学和量子引力效应。现有研究缺乏在dS时空中均匀电场与平行磁场共同作用下精确的单圈有效作用量，以及其对外部场下耦合常数演化（如牛顿引力常数）的影响。

Method: 采用gamma函数正则化方法处理in-out形式体系，在固有时积分表示中精确推导均匀电场与平行磁场下带电标量场的单圈有效作用量。通过与Minkowski时空极限（还原为Weisskopf-Schwinger作用量）和纯dS时空极限的对比，系统分析不同极限情形下的有效耦合常数行为，以及有效势中真空极化振幅的前导贡献。

Result: 得到了dS时空中均匀电场与平行磁场下精确的单圈有效作用量，并还原为Minkowski时空的Weisskopf-Schwinger结果和纯dS时空结果。纯强电场极限下耦合常数的有效强度呈现出重要发现，进一步佐证了牛顿引力常数在外部场影响下的演化。有效势分析表明，依赖于非最小耦合阈值的真空极化前导贡献可赋予磁场非零真空期望值，产生大尺度磁场。

Conclusion: 该研究为外部场诱导的引力常数演化提供了新证据，并提出了一个原初磁生成机制：在dS膨胀早期阶段，真空极化效应可为磁场提供非零真空期望值，从而产生大磁场，为理解早期宇宙的磁生成问题提供了新的理论路径。

Abstract: Particles creation and vacuum polarization under the influence of both an electromagnetic field and a de~Sitter (dS) spacetime nonperturbatively probes quantum electrodynamics (QED) and quantum gravitational effects. By applying the gamma-function regularization to the in-out formulation, we find the exact one-loop effective action in the proper-time integral representation for a charged scalar field in a uniform electric field and a parallel magnetic field in a dS spacetime. It reduces to the one-loop scalar QED Weisskopf-Schwinger effective action in the limit of Minkowski spacetime and the one-loop effective action in the pure dS spacetime. After carefully considering the different limiting cases, a striking result concerns the effective strengths of the coupling constants in the pure strong electric field limit. Our study provides further evidence for evolution of Newton's gravitational constant under the influence of the external fields. Remarkably, the analysis of the effective potential shows that the leading order contributions to the vacuum polarization amplitude, depending on the nonminimal coupling threshold, give a nonzero vacuum expectation value of the magnetic field. This mechanism generates a large magnetic field in the early dS stage of the expansion that may imply primordial magnetogenesis.

</details>


### [145] [The Hydrotope in the Water-Wave Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26881)
*Qu Cao,Song He,Jirong Jing,Qiupeng Li*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Hydrotope几何（水波振幅的几何表示）直接源于水波作用量本身。通过将n点接触相互作用写成特定形式，证明了特定符号配置下的系数正是Hydrotope体积的两倍，并通过切割两负分支树的唯一顶点对所有两负树求和，恢复了已知振幅公式，表明该几何已局部编码在水波作用量中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hydrotope此前为树级水波振幅提供了优雅的几何表示（超平面切片盒子的体积），但该几何的物理起源尚不明朗。本文旨在追溯这一几何结构在水波作用量中的直接来源，揭示其并非偶然的数学巧合，而是作用量中固有的局部结构，并指向更广泛的水波振幅中隐藏的几何简单性。

Method: (1) 将n点接触相互作用重写为V_n=Σ w_i w_j h_ij^(n)的形式，并证明当两个标记动量为同号且所有旁观动量异号时，系数为h_ij^(n)=2H_n（其中H_n/(n-3)!为Hydrotope体积）。(2) 证明任意固定对的系数具有无分母的有序旗表示，即(n-3)维盒子体积的有向和。(3) 通过在连接两个负分支的唯一定点上切割，对所有两负树求和，分别计算减减、减加、加加频率双线性项前的系数，得到(2^(n-1)-2)H_n、0和2H_n。

Result: 恢复出已知振幅2^(n-1)w_1w_2H_n。这一结果证明了Hydrotope几何及更广的盒子切片几何已直接局部编码于水波作用量中，而非仅作为事后重构的数学工具。

Conclusion: Hydrotope几何的起源可直接追溯至水波作用量中的接触相互作用系数，这暗示了一般水波振幅中存在进一步的隐藏简单性与几何结构，为后续探索提供了新方向。

Abstract: The Hydrotope gives a geometric representation of tree-level water-wave amplitudes with two negative spatial momenta as the volume of a hyperplane slice of a box. We trace the origin of this geometry directly to the water-wave action. Writing the $n$-point contact interaction as $\mathcal{V}_n=\sum_{i<j}w_iw_jh_{ij}^{(n)}$, we show that when the two marked momenta have the same sign and every spectator has the opposite sign, the corresponding coefficient is $h_{ij}^{(n)}=2H_n$, where $H_n/(n{-}3)!$ is the Hydrotope volume. More generally, every fixed-pair coefficient admits a denominator-free ordered-flag representation as an oriented sum of $(n{-}3)$-dimensional box volumes. We then sum all two-minus trees by cutting each at the unique vertex joining its two minus branches. The coefficients multiplying minus-minus, minus-plus, and plus-plus frequency bilinears reduce, respectively, to $(2^{n{-}1}{-}2)H_n$, $0$, and $2H_n$, and immediately reproduce the known amplitude $2^{n{-}1}w_1w_2H_n$. Thus, the Hydrotope-and a broader class of related box-slice geometries-is already encoded locally in the water-wave action. This points to further hidden simplicity and geometric structure in general water-wave amplitudes.

</details>


### [146] [Testing holographic computation of entanglement pseudo-entropy in dS\textsubscript{3}/ICFT\textsubscript{2}](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26997)
*Liang Li*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用Coleman-De Luccia瞬子构建了一个从引力侧研究"虚拟"de-Sitter/界面CFT（dS/ICFT）对应的自下而上模型。将暴胀场剧烈变化的区域吸收到膜上后，时空由两个半径不同的de Sitter时空与膜连接而成，预期对偶于界面CFT。作者分析了现有的全息计算伪熵（极值曲面）的方案，指出其中一些方案会导致与界面CFT普适性质相矛盾的悖论，并通过解析延拓与AdS/ICFT对应结果进行对比，还从垂直于界面的子区域的纠缠熵中读出g函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立dS/ICFT对应的具体引力模型，并检验现有全息伪熵计算方案的合理性，发现其中可能存在的悖论，同时与AdS/ICFT的结果进行对照，以深化对dS/CFT猜想的理解。

Method: 采用Coleman-De Luccia瞬子作为自下而上构建的框架，用膜吸收暴胀场剧烈变化区域，形成由两个不同半径的de Sitter时空经膜拼接的几何；在该时空中计算极值曲面的伪熵，与AdS/ICFT解析延拓结果比较，并从垂直于界面的子区域纠缠熵提取g函数。

Result: 发现某些现有的伪熵全息计算方案在dS/ICFT框架下会与界面CFT的普适性质（如g函数行为）产生矛盾，通过解析延拓与AdS/ICFT的对比揭示了两者之间的关联与差异；成功从界面垂直子区域的纠缠熵中读出g函数。

Conclusion: 该工作为dS/ICFT对应提供了可操作的引力侧模型，指出了现有伪熵计算的潜在问题，并为未来在dS背景下研究界面CFT的普适性质提供了新的理论工具和约束。

Abstract: We establish a bottom-up model for studying the `would-be' de-Sitter/interface CFT (dS/ICFT) correspondence on the gravity side, using the Coleman-De Luccia instanton. After absorbing the region where the inflaton changes dramatically by a brane, the spacetime consists of two de Sitter spacetimes with different radii joined by a brane, which is expected to be dual to an interface CFT provided that the dS/CFT conjecture is correct. Our goal is to analyze existing prescriptions for the holographic computation of pseudo-entropy, i.e. the extremal surface in such a spacetime and argue that some of them would cause parodox with the results expected the universal properties of interface CFT and compare our results with that of Anti de Sitter/interface CFT correspondence (AdS/ICFT) after analytic continuation. We also read off the $g$-function from the entanglement entropy associated with a subregion perpendicular to the interface.

</details>


### [147] [Wess-Zumino gauge for analytic prepotentials of off-shell $\mathcal{N}=2$ supergravity: bosonic sector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27308)
*Nikita Zaigraev,Julia Zernina*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了$\mathcal{N}=2$ Weyl超重力和$\mathcal{N}=2$ Einstein超重力（含非线性张量补偿子版本）的解析预势的Wess-Zumino规范，推导了场重定义、完整变换律和保持该规范的规范超参数，并将其应用于自由超多重态作用量耦合$\mathcal{N}=2$超重力的分量结构分析（仅限玻色子扇区）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在$\mathcal{N}=2$超重力中，解析预势的Wess-Zumino规范是提取分量形式的关键，但此前对这一规范在不同超重力版本中的具体实现和保持条件缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 对离壳$\mathcal{N}=2$ Weyl超重力和含非线性张量补偿子的$\mathcal{N}=2$ Einstein超重力的解析预势进行Wess-Zumino规范分析，推导场重定义与保持该规范的规范超参数，并将结果应用于自由超多重态作用量。

Result: 给出了两种超重力版本中Wess-Zumino规范下的完整变换律和保持规范所需的超参数条件，并获得了耦合$\mathcal{N}=2$超重力的自由超多重态作用量的玻色子分量结构。

Conclusion: 该研究为$\mathcal{N}=2$超重力理论的分量级分析提供了系统的Wess-Zumino规范框架，为后续完整超对称分量结构和量子层面研究奠定基础。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive study of the Wess-Zumino gauge for the analytic prepotentials of the off-shell $\mathcal{N}=2$ Weyl and $\mathcal{N}=2$ Einstein supergravities (the version with a non-linear tensor compensator). The field redefinitions, the full transformation laws, and the gauge superparameters that preserve the Wess-Zumino gauge are derived. As a concrete application of the obtained results, we examine the component structure of the free hypermultiplet action coupled to $\mathcal{N}=2$ supergravity. In this work, we restrict our analysis strictly to the bosonic sector.

</details>


### [148] [Quantum Selection of Classical Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27388)
*Omer Guleryuz*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过超引力实现中的暴胀模型，证明有限量子事件（有限时域的首过过程）可以使量子涨落导致的相空间波动进入经典确定性演化无法到达的竞争性经典延续，从而在多个经典未来之间产生持久性重分配，即使这些经典未来本身是预先存在的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨量子涨落能否在预制备态无法确定性到达的经典未来中留下持久影响，即有限量子剧集是否能在多个经典延续间产生不可逆的选择或重分配。

Method: 以暴胀作为宇宙学实验室，在超引力实现中构建两个经典延续，其中一个在没有噪声情况下可到达，另一个不可到达。使稳定熵方向暂时变轻（但不产生tachyonic不稳定性），让模式导出的相空间涨落进入竞争延续，利用有限时域首过过程分配条件权重，并对比抑制随机源时权重恢复为零。

Result: 在三个CMB归一化实现中，竞争延续被赋予0.33-0.40的条件权重，而抑制随机源时精确恢复确定性零值。随后熵扇区重新变重，被选中的历史继续经典演化。

Conclusion: 有限量子剧集可以对经典未来产生持久的重分配效应，打破纯确定性演化的限制，为量子—经典过渡中的历史选择机制提供了新认识。

Abstract: Can a finite quantum episode populate a classical future that the same prepared state never reaches deterministically? We show that it can, using inflation as a cosmological laboratory. In our supergravity realization, two classical continuations already exist, but the prepared state reaches only one without noise. A stable entropy direction temporarily becomes light without a tachyonic instability, allowing mode-derived phase-space fluctuations to access the competing continuation. Finite-horizon first passage assigns it a \(0.33\)--\(0.40\) conditional weight across three nearby CMB-normalized realizations, while suppressing the stochastic source restores its exact deterministic zero. The entropy sector then becomes heavy again, and the selected histories continue classically. A finite quantum episode can thus leave a lasting redistribution among classical futures.

</details>


### [149] [Doubly-scaled planar ${\cal N} = 4$ SYM \& Carroll Holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27410)
*Arjun Bagchi,Prateksh Dhivakar,Alok Laddha,Partha Paul*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文展示了N=4超对称杨-米尔斯理论中四点关联函数的双缩放极限可以映射到未来零无穷远I⁺上，从而定义4维Carrollian共形场论，并扩展到更高点函数和1/2 BPS扇区的递推关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Okuda和Penedones证明了N=4 SYM四点关联函数的双缩放极限与R^{1,9}中树级弦振幅的平坦空间极限对偶。本文旨在将这些关联函数映射到渐近平坦时空的共形边界（未来零无穷远），从而建立Carrollian CFT的新构造机制。

Method: 1) 将N=4 SYM双缩放极限下的关联函数映射到I⁺，得到4d Carrollian CFT；2) 将OP分析扩展到更高点函数，利用Gram条件约束双缩放极限；3) 利用膨胀弦振幅的软因子化定理，为1/2 BPS扇区建立递推关系；4) 提出一般性工具包，通过平坦空间振幅的积分变换构造Carrollian关联函数。

Result: 成功证明在无限't Hooft耦合极限下N=4 SYM理论在双缩放极限中涌现出Carrollian CFT；将分析推广到更高点关联函数；建立了1/2 BPS扇区的递推关系；提出了从非引力有效场论平坦空间振幅构造Carrollian CFT的通用方法。

Conclusion: 双缩放极限是构造Carrollian CFT的系统性框架，为研究渐近平坦时空全息原理与Carrollian共形场论提供了新途径。

Abstract: Okuda and Penedones (OP) \cite{Okuda:2010ym} showed the existence of a double scaling limit of four point correlators in ${\cal N}=4$ Supersymmetric Yang Mills (SYM) theory in four spacetime dimensions (4$d$) dual to the flat space limit of tree-level string amplitude in $\mathbb{R}^{1,9}$ with external kinematics in $\mathbb{R}^{1,4}$. In this paper, we show that the resulting boundary correlators can be used to define a 4d Carrollian conformal field theory (CFT) on future null infinity (${\I}^{+}$). This is done by mapping the set of correlators in ${\cal N} = 4$ SYM theory (in the double scaling limit) to ${\I}^{+}$, which is the conformal boundary of all asymptotically flat spacetimes. A Carroll CFT thus emerges in the infinite 't Hooft coupling limit of ${\cal N} = 4$ SYM theory. We extend the OP analysis to higher point functions and show how the Gram conditions on flat space amplitude put constraints on the double scaling limit of SYM correlators. We then use the soft factorization theorem for dilatonic string amplitude to write a recursion relation for $\frac{1}{2}$ BPS sector of ${\cal N} = 4$ SYM theory in the double scaling limit. Finally, we show that the essential ideas underlying such a double scaling limit can be used as a tool-kit to build a class of Carrollian correlators, and hence define a Carrollian CFT, via certain integral transforms of flat space amplitudes of non-gravitational effective field theories.

</details>


### [150] [Casimir operators of $4D\,, \mathcal{N}=2$ supersymmetry in the harmonic approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27412)
*Egor Eremeev,Evgeny Ivanov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文在谐和超空间方法中构造了4维N=2超对称代数的Casimir算子，给出了解析基下协变导数的显式表达式。关键结果是超级同位旋算子C_I用新的'长'谐和导数表达，这些导数包含逆Box算子的非局域项并满足修正的SU(2)代数关系。同时构造了适用于零质量情形的N=2超螺旋性和超等螺旋性算子，并通过若干例子计算了其特征值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在4D N=2超对称理论中，Casimir算子是分类超场表示和描述理论动力学结构的重要工具。以往对N=1情形的Casimir算子已有深入研究，但N=2情形下在谐和超空间框架中构造Casimir算子仍是一个待解决的问题，这对于理解N=2超场理论的分类和相互作用具有重要意义。

Method: 采用谐和超空间（harmonic superspace）方法，通过定义包含逆Box算子Box^{-1}的'长'谐和导数nabla^{+-}, nabla^{--}, nabla^0来构造Casimir算子。超级同位旋算子C_I被表达为这些长谐和导数的组合。这些导数满足修正的SU(2)代数关系，其中nabla^0在解析超场上的特征值相对于标准U(1)谐和荷偏移-2。进一步构造N=2超螺旋性和超等螺旋性算子用于质量为零（Box=0）的情形。

Result: 成功构造了4D N=2超对称代数的Casimir算子，特别是超级同位旋算子C_I = (1/4)(nabla^0)^2 + (1/2){nabla^{++}, nabla^{--}}，其中长谐和导数含非局域逆Box项且满足修正的SU(2)代数。同时构造了N=2超螺旋性和超等螺旋性算子，并在若干4D N=2超场理论例子（如超场模型）中计算了其特征值。

Conclusion: 该工作在谐和超空间框架下完成了4D N=2超对称代数Casimir算子的构造，为N=2超场理论的分类和动力学分析提供了新的数学工具。非局域逆Box项的引入和修正的SU(2)代数关系揭示了N=2情形与N=1情形的本质差异，超螺旋性算子的构造为质量零情形的物理分析奠定了基础。

Abstract: We construct Casimir operators of $4D, \,\mathcal{N}=2$ supersymmetry algebra in the harmonic superspace approach, and provide the explicit expressions for them in terms of covariant derivatives in the analytic basis. Specifically, the superisospin operator $C_I$ is expressed in terms of new 'long' harmonic derivatives $\nabla^{\pm\pm}, \nabla^0$ as $C_I = \frac{1}{4}\left(\nabla^0\right)^2 + \frac{1}{2}\left\{\nabla^{++},\nabla^{--}\right\}$. These derivatives involve non-local terms with the inverse Box operator $\Box^{-1}$ and satisfy modified $SU(2)$ algebra relations in which the eigenvalues of $\nabla^0$ on analytic superfields are shifted by -2 relative to the standard $U(1)$ harmonic charges. We also construct $\mathcal{N}=2$ superhelicity and super-isohelicity operators relevant to the massless case (with vanishing $\Box$) and find their eigenvalues for few instructive examples of $4D, \,\mathcal{N}=2$ superfield theories.

</details>


### [151] [Proof of the AGT Conjecture at Generic $β$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27447)
*Le-Feng Chen,Kilar Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文给出了四点半超多重态、一般β参数下SU(2) AGT对应的全级数证明，通过证明广义Jack多项式Selberg平均的全级数分解公式，结合广义Kadell公式与广义Cauchy恒等式，将每个双分拆项与Nekrasov不动点贡献精确对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前AGT对应虽然在有限级数上得到验证，但缺乏全级数的严格证明。特别是广义Kadell公式仅在有限级数上被验证，需要一个完整的解析证明来确立一般β参数下四点SU(2) AGT对应的严格成立。

Method: 1) 通过广义Macdonald Pieri规则的Jack极限（逐系数取极限）获得严格Cauchy对偶基中的单盒矩阵元；2) 利用有理角函数恒等式求和对所有父双分拆；3) 通过对Selberg核的显式全导数得到Dotsenko-Fateev电荷平衡超平面上的三角递归；4) 该递归的唯一解即推广的Kadell公式；5) 结合广义Cauchy恒等式将每个双分拆项与Nekrasov不动点贡献一一对应。

Result: 成功证明了广义Kadell公式（此前仅在有限级数上验证），进而给出了四点半超多重态、一般β参数下SU(2) AGT对应的完整全级数证明。

Conclusion: 通过全级数分解公式和三角递归方法，首次为一般β参数的SU(2) AGT对应提供了严格的全级数证明，填补了此前仅有限级数验证的空白。

Abstract: We give an all-level proof of the four-point $SU(2)$ AGT correspondence with four fundamental hypermultiplets at generic $β=-ε_1/ε_2$, by proving an all-level factorization formula for Selberg averages of generalized Jack polynomials. Taking a coefficientwise Jack limit of the generalized Macdonald Pieri rule, we obtain the required one-box matrix elements in the strict Cauchy dual basis. A rational corner-function identity then evaluates the sum over all parent double partitions, while an explicit total derivative of the Selberg kernel yields a triangular recursion on the Dotsenko-Fateev charge balance hyperplane. The unique solution of this recursion is the generalized Kadell formula previously verified only through finite level. Combining this result with the generalized Cauchy identity identifies each double partition term with the corresponding Nekrasov fixed-point contribution.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>
