### [152] [Classical and Hybrid Quantum Machine Learning for Trigger-Like Event Selection on CMS Open Data: An Eight-Qubit, PCA-Constrained Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26224)
*Tariq Mahmood,Muhammad Awais Rafique,Talab Hussain,Juan Pablo Perez Aguilar,Alfredo Raya,Muhammad Ahsan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在高能物理触发判别任务中系统比较了四种经典机器学习模型（SVM、ANN、CNN、LSTM）与四种混合量子模型（量子核、量子神经网络、量子卷积网络、量子循环网络），基于CMS开放数据构建不变质量窗口二分类任务。结果显示最强的经典模型为ANN（93.53%准确率），最强的量子模型为QCNN（90.89%准确率），研究定位为受控基准参考而非量子优势声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能物理事件触发处于实验核心地位，需在极端延迟和带宽限制下从海量背景中保留稀有信号事件。现有研究缺乏在同一严格约束下对经典与量子机器学习模型进行公平、系统对比的控制性基准，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 基于CMS开放数据构建触发式二分类任务，标签由不变质量窗口定义；输入特征包含重建运动学参数及物理衍生变量（伪快度差、方位角差、角距离和总横动量）。所有模型共享相同的分层划分、预处理和决策阈值。量子模型受限于8量子比特固定资源预算、16维主成分压缩和态矢量模拟。性能通过准确率、ROC-AUC、F1分数、精确率和召回率综合评估。

Result: 经典模型中ANN表现最佳（93.53%准确率，0.9819 ROC-AUC）；量子模型中QCNN最强（90.89%准确率，0.9731 ROC-AUC），QNN紧随其后。量子核与量子循环方法表现落后，表明在此预算下可训练的混合嵌入方法更具优势。

Conclusion: 该研究提供一个受控的比较参考点，而非声称量子优势。在当前8量子比特预算下，经典模型总体优于量子对应模型，但量子卷积网络已展现出接近经典性能的潜力，可作为未来量子计算硬件发展后的基线参考。

Abstract: Event triggering sits at the heart of high-energy physics, where the rare events of interest must be retained while an overwhelming background is discarded under tight latency and bandwidth budgets. This work compares four classical machine learning models, namely a support vector machine, an artificial neural network, a convolutional network and a long short-term memory network, with four hybrid quantum counterparts, on a trigger-like binary classification task built from CMS open data. The label is defined by an invariant-mass window, and the inputs combine reconstructed kinematics with physics-motivated derived variables: the pseudorapidity difference, the wrapped azimuthal difference, the angular separation and the total transverse momentum. The quantum models run under a fixed resource budget of eight qubits, a principal-component compression to sixteen features and state-vector simulation. Every model shares the same stratified split, the same preprocessing and a common decision threshold, and performance is reported through accuracy, ROC-AUC, F1-score, precision and recall. The strongest classical model is the artificial neural network, at 93.53 percent accuracy and 0.9819 ROC-AUC, while the strongest quantum model is the quantum convolutional network, at 90.89 percent accuracy and 0.9731 ROC-AUC, with the quantum neural network close behind. The quantum-kernel and recurrent quantum approaches trail both, which places the trainable hybrid embeddings ahead within this budget. The study is meant as a controlled reference point rather than a claim of quantum advantage.

</details>


### [153] [FlaSR: A Mathematica Package for the Automatic Generation of $U$-spin Sum Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26248)
*Margarita Gavrilova,Livia Kong*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍FlaSR，一个基于Mathematica的软件包，用于自动生成高阶幅度和幅度平方的SU(2)味求和规则，特别关注U-spin对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 味求和规则在粒子物理中具有重要应用，但手动推导高阶对称性破缺下的完备求和规则非常繁琐且容易出错，需要自动化工具来提高效率和准确性。

Method: FlaSR基于对味求和规则数学结构的最新理解，用户输入初末态粒子多重态列表以及对称极限哈密顿量的变换性质，软件包自动生成完整的过程列表，并输出幅度求和规则（在存在此类关系的所有对称性破缺阶上生成完备集合）和幅度平方求和规则（当前版本不保证完备性）。

Result: 软件包能够为幅度求和规则在所有阶生成完备集合，为幅度平方求和规则生成（但不保证完备的）集合，并通过一系列代表性示例展示了其适用范围。

Conclusion: FlaSR为味求和规则的自动推导提供了实用工具，尤其适用于U-spin相关的分析，但幅度平方求和规则的完备性仍需未来版本改进。

Abstract: We present FlaSR, a Mathematica package for the automatic generation of higher-order amplitude and amplitude-squared $SU(2)$ flavor sum rules, with a particular emphasis on $U$-spin. The package is based on recent insights into the mathematical structure of flavor sum rules. Given a system of symmetry-related processes defined by a list of particle multiplets in the initial and final states, together with the transformation properties of the symmetry-limit Hamiltonian, the package generates the full list of processes and outputs amplitude and amplitude-squared sum rules for the system. For amplitude sum rules, the package generates the complete set at all orders in symmetry breaking for which such relations exist. For amplitude-squared sum rules, which translate directly into sum rules among physical observables, the current version of the package is not guaranteed to generate the complete set. We illustrate the scope of the package with a range of representative examples.

</details>


### [154] [OperatorToC++: Transpiling Matched EFT Coefficients to Low-Level Routines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26254)
*Sabine Kraml,Andre Lessa,Suraj Prakash,Felix Wilsch*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为OperatorToC++的混合工具，它结合Mathematica和C++的优势，将新物理模型与低能有效场论匹配后得到的解析表达式（包含复杂的圈函数和冗长的张量求和/乘积项）自动翻译并打包为C++类和函数，并支持通过Python调用已编译的Wilson系数方法，从而为后续数值分析和与高能物理Python生态系统的集成提供便利平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管自动匹配工具已取得显著进展，但匹配后的解析表达式仍包含复杂的圈函数和涉及张量对象的冗长求和/乘积项，给进一步的数值分析带来了挑战，需要一个能够高效处理这些复杂表达式并将其转化为可方便用于数值计算代码的工具。

Method: OperatorToC++采用可扩展的混合架构，结合Mathematica的符号计算能力和C++的高性能数值计算能力。该工具首先高效处理匹配解析表达式中的复杂圈函数和长求和/乘积项，然后将结果翻译并打包为C++类和函数，最后提供将编译后的Wilson系数方法作为Python函数调用的接口。

Result: OperatorToC++成功地将匹配后的解析表达式转换为C++代码，提供了便捷的数值分析平台，并通过Python绑定实现了与高能物理Python库生态系统的无缝链接。

Conclusion: OperatorToC++填补了自动匹配工具与后续数值分析之间的空白，通过混合C++/Python/Mathematica架构为高能物理工作流提供了完整且灵活的解决方案。

Abstract: In recent years, significant progress has been made in the development of automated tools that match the parameters of new physics models and the appropriate low-energy Effective Field Theories. This work introduces an extensible, hybrid tool, OperatorToC++, that combines the strengths of Mathematica and C++ to facilitate the next steps beyond the matching. OperatorToC++ efficiently tackles the complexities within the analytical matched expressions such as intricate loop-functions and lengthy sums and products involving tensor objects. It then translates and bundles the results into C++ classes and functions which provide a convenient platform for further numerical analyses. Finally, it offers the possibility of calling the compiled Wilson coefficient methods as Python functions, thus enabling to link them with the vast Python library ecosystem for High Energy Physics workflows.

</details>


### [155] [First-Principles Nuclear Modeling for Light Dark Matter Experiments at the Intensity Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26255)
*Taylor R. Gray,Alberto Scalesi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了将多体ab initio方法应用于电子固定靶实验中轻暗物质中介子产生信号率的计算，使用手征有效场论相互作用，对三种代表性原子核（20Ne、34Si、56Fe）在弹性与非弹性散射两种情况下计算了截面，并与常用唯象参数化对比，发现准弹性处理可将信号产额提高最多两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强度前沿的加速器实验（高能束流轰击核靶）是探测轻暗物质范式的有力手段，此类实验需要对靶核进行精确建模才能获得可靠的信号预测，而传统唯象方法可能存在较大偏差。

Method: 采用多体ab initio方法（基于手征有效场论相互作用），结合ab initio核弹性形状因子和谱函数，在Monte Carlo事件生成器中实现，对20Ne、34Si、56Fe三种原子核在变电子束能量下计算弹性与非弹性散射截面。

Result: 与常用唯象参数化相比，准弹性散射处理使信号产额提高最多两个数量级；对于较轻的中介子，两种方法结果一致。

Conclusion: ab initio方法对固定靶轻暗物质搜索实验的信号预测至关重要，尤其在高动量转移的准弹性区域，传统唯象参数化会显著低估信号率。

Abstract: Accelerator-based experiments at the intensity frontier, in which a high-energy beam impinges on a nuclear target, serve as powerful probes of the light dark matter paradigm. Such experiments require precise modeling of the target nucleus for reliable signal predictions. We present the application of a many-body ab initio method to calculate light dark matter mediator production signal rates at electron fixed-target experiments, using chiral effective field theory interactions. Considering both elastic and quasi elastic scattering, we compute cross sections using a Monte Carlo event generator implementation informed by ab initio nuclear elastic form factors and spectral functions for three representative nuclei, $^{20}$Ne, $^{34}$Si, and $^{56}$Fe, at varying electron beam energies. We compare our results to a commonly used phenomenological parameterization, finding an increased signal yield by up to two orders of magnitude with our quasi elastic treatment and an agreement for lighter mediators.

</details>


### [156] [Getting Warmer: IceCube Nears Freeze Out](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26258)
*Mainak Mukhopadhyay,Gordan Krnjaic*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文利用IceCube最近探测到的来自银河系的弥散高能中微子，通过两种互补方法（基于银经分布的背景扣除法和基于内银河系能谱的模板依赖法）对暗物质湮灭到中微子的截面设置了新限制，结果比现有约束改善数倍，并证明银河系中微子观测可作为暗物质湮灭的有力探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IceCube近期高显著性探测到来自银河系的弥散高能中微子，这为利用银河系中微子辐射探测暗物质湮灭提供了新的机会。此前基于银河系中微子的暗物质约束较弱或依赖于特定的宇宙线发射模型，作者希望利用这一新探测结果对暗物质湮灭截面施加更强的限制。

Method: 采用两种互补方法：(1) 利用背景扣除后的shower事件银经分布，对暗物质湮灭截面施加不依赖任何银河宇宙线发射模型的保守约束；(2) 利用在不同银河宇宙线发射模型下推断的内银河系模板依赖中微子能谱，检验其对暗物质贡献的敏感性。

Result: 方法一得到的速度平均湮灭截面限制比现有约束改善数倍；方法二表明在TeV质量尺度范围内，推断的银河系中微子强度已对接近热遗迹基准的暗物质贡献敏感，尽管更具模型依赖性。

Conclusion: 银河系弥散中微子发射测量可作为探测暗物质湮灭到中微子的强大工具。未来IceCube-Gen2和KM3NeT的观测将大幅扩展灵敏度，有望通过银河系中微子观测对热冻出机制进行决定性检验。

Abstract: IceCube has recently detected a diffuse population of high-energy neutrinos arising from the Milky Way. We use this high-significance detection to place new limits on dark matter (DM) annihilation to neutrinos with two complementary approaches. The first method uses the background-subtracted Galactic longitude distribution of shower events to place a conservative bound on the DM annihilation cross section that does not rely on any assumed Galactic cosmic ray emission model; the resulting limits on the velocity-averaged annihilation cross section improve upon existing bounds by factors of a few. The second method uses the template-dependent neutrino energy spectra from the Inner Galaxy, inferred under different Galactic cosmic ray emission models. This complementary approach shows that the inferred Galactic neutrino intensities are already sensitive to DM contributions near the thermal-relic benchmark for a range of TeV-scale DM masses, though this comparison is more model-dependent. Our results demonstrate that measurements of diffuse Galactic neutrino emission can be used as a powerful probe of DM annihilation into neutrinos. Future observations with IceCube-Gen2 and KM3NeT will substantially extend this sensitivity, potentially allowing a decisive test of the thermal freeze-out mechanism with Galactic neutrino observations.

</details>


### [157] [Enhancement of nonlinear pair production in a flying-focus pulse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26313)
*Md Reshad Ur Rahman,Martin S. Formanek,Elias Gerstmayr,Dillon Ramsey,John P. Palastro,Antonino Di Piazza*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了飞行聚焦（flying-focus, FF）激光脉冲在非线性Breit-Wheeler对产生（NBWPP）中的应用，发现FF脉冲比传统的静态聚焦高斯（SFG）脉冲能更有效地产生电子-正电子对，在10、20和50 GeV光子能量下分别实现对产生产额12%、29%和76%的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能伽马光子在强激光脉冲中可通过非线性Breit-Wheeler过程衰变为电子-正电子对。在足够强的脉冲下，NBWPP概率随相互作用时间的增长比随场强增长更为有利。传统静态聚焦高斯（SFG）激光脉冲的焦点位置固定，限制了与光子束的相互作用时间，因此需要探索具有可编程焦点速度的脉冲来提升对产生产额。

Method: 采用飞行聚焦（FF）脉冲方案：FF脉冲与高能光子束相向传播，同时其焦点以光速沿光束方向运动，从而延长光子与强场的有效相互作用时间。通过数值模拟，将等能量紧聚焦的焦耳级FF脉冲与SFG脉冲的对产生产额进行对比，分别针对10、20和50 GeV光子进行模拟测试。

Result: 数值模拟表明，与等能量SFG脉冲相比，焦耳级紧聚焦FF脉冲在10、20和50 GeV光子能量下分别将电子-正电子对产生产额提升了12%、29%和76%。

Conclusion: 在该参数区间内，FF脉冲比传统SFG脉冲更高效地产生电子-正电子对，为提高NBWPP产额提供了一种新的激光脉冲设计途径。

Abstract: A photon can decay into an electron-positron pair in the presence of an intense laser pulse by a process known as nonlinear Breit-Wheeler pair production (NBWPP). For a sufficiently intense pulse, the probability of NBWPP for a high-energy gamma photon scales more favorably with the interaction time than with the field intensity. In contrast to typical stationary-focus Gaussian (SFG) laser pulses, a flying-focus (FF) pulse features a focal point that moves at a programmable velocity. Here, we show that a FF pulse counterpropagating with a high-energy photon beam while its focus copropagates with the beam at the speed of light enhances the NBWPP yield relative to an equal-energy SFG pulse. Numerical simulations show that a Joule-class tightly-focused FF pulse allows for an enhancement of the pair-production yield by 12\%, 29\% and 76\% for a photon of 10, 20 and 50 GeV energy, respectively. Thus, in this regime, FF pulses are more efficient at producing electron-positron pairs than conventional SFG pulses.

</details>


### [158] [Direct and Indirect searches for DM-electron interactions in sub-GeV DM models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26467)
*Marco Cirelli,Arpan Kar,Antoine Letessier-Selvon,Harrys Lumengo-Kidimbu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析了亚GeV暗物质直接探测（DD）与间接探测（ID）之间的互补性，聚焦于两个现实模型：矢量门户（暗光子）和标量门户（希格斯门户）模型。研究发现矢量门户模型中直接探测与间接探测的互补性最佳，尤其在重介质子情况下DAMIC-M可直接与间接探测竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚GeV暗物质的探测策略需要在直接探测与间接探测之间进行系统比较和互补性评估，以确定最佳的探测窗口和策略。

Method: 在矢量门户和标量门户两个现实模型中，采用DAMIC-M实验的暗物质-电子散射约束及未来预期，结合X射线/伽马射线和宇宙射线正负电子对的现有观测及未来COSI MeV望远镜的预期，将暗物质湮灭率约束转化为电子散射截面并比较。

Result: 矢量门户模型下直接探测与间接探测互补性最佳，特别是在重介质子情况中，现有DAMIC-M界限在3-20 MeV暗物质质量范围内可与间接探测综合限制相当或更优；其他情况下间接探测通常更具约束力，且在部分质量范围内能约束中微子地板以下的参数空间。

Conclusion: 直接探测与间接探测的互补性取决于暗物质模型和介质子质量关系；间接探测在多数情况下更敏感，但直接探测在特定模型和质量区间内能提供竞争性约束，两者结合可扩展探测覆盖范围。

Abstract: We analyze the complementarity between direct detection (DD) and indirect detection (ID) searches of sub-GeV dark matter (DM) in the context of two realistic models: the vector-portal (dark photon) and scalar-portal (higgs-portal) models. For DD we focus on the latest constraints on the DM-electron scattering from the present DAMIC-M experiment as well as its future projection. For ID we consider several existing X-rays/gamma-rays and cosmic-ray ($e^\pm$) observations as well as the upcoming MeV telescope COSI to obtain the corresponding bounds and projections on the DM annihilation rate, which are then translated in terms of the DM-electron scattering cross-section and compared with the DD bounds/projections. We properly take into account astrophysical and galactic propagation uncertainties. We find that the complementarity between DAMIC-M and ID works best for the vector-portal model, especially for the case where the vector mediator is heavier than the DM particle: in this case, the present DAMIC-M bound becomes comparable to (or better than, for some astrophysical configurations) the combined ID limit for $3\,{\rm MeV} \lesssim m_{\rm DM} \lesssim 20\,{\rm MeV}$. For other cases and mass ranges, the ID is in general more constraining and can also, for some mass ranges, constrain the parameter space below the neutrino floor, which is inaccessible to DD experiments.

</details>


### [159] [Atomic structure calculations for constraining new electron-electron forces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26532)
*Cameron J. West,Benjamin M. Roberts*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种计算原子结构理论中新型电子-电子相互作用的通用方法，通过时间依赖Hartree-Fock方法纳入多体效应，并利用铯原子电偶极矩和宇称不守恒跃迁的实验数据，对标量-赝标量和矢量-轴矢量相互作用的耦合强度给出了新的界限，覆盖了此前未限定的质量区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在发展一种更通用、更精确的方法来计算原子中新型电子-电子相互作用，以改进先前只考虑单粒子效应而忽略多体修正的计算，从而为超出标准模型的新物理提供更严格的约束界限。

Method: 采用时间依赖Hartree-Fock（TDHF）方法引入多体修正，该方法适用于任意中介子质量；对于标量-赝标量相互作用，结合原子电偶极矩实验数据提取界限；对于矢量-轴矢量相互作用，利用原子的宇称不守恒（PNC）跃迁实验数据提取耦合强度界限；同时更新了标准模型中Z⁰交换对铯6s-7s PNC跃迁振幅E_PNC的贡献计算。

Result: 对矢量-轴矢量相互作用给出了严格的界限：在中介子质量m_X高于约10 MeV时，耦合-质量比|g_V^e g_A^e|/m_X²不能大于10⁻¹¹ MeV⁻²，覆盖了此前实验中未被约束的质量区域。此外，还更新了铯原子标准模型PNC跃迁振幅E_PNC中Z⁰交换贡献的计算。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法提供了一种在任意中介子质量下计算新型电子-电子相互作用并纳入多体效应的通用框架，成功填补了先前参数空间中的未约束区域，为新物理搜索提供了更严格的原子物理界限。

Abstract: We present a general approach for calculating new electron-electron interactions with atomic structure theory, include dominant many-body effects, and extract bounds for parity-odd cases. The procedure is valid for any mediator mass and improves previous calculations by including many-body corrections to the new interaction through the time-dependent Hartree-Fock method. Bounds on coupling strengths are then extracted for scalar-pseudoscalar and vector-axial vector interactions by comparison with experimental atomic electric dipole moments and parity non-conserving (PNC) transitions respectively. In the latter case, our calculations rule out any coupling-mass ratio |g^e_V g^e_A|/m_X^2 greater than 10^-11 MeV^-2 above m_X ~ 10 MeV, covering a previously unconstrained mass region. We also report an updated calculation of the regular (Standard Model) electron-electron Z^0 exchange contribution to the 6s-7s PNC transition amplitude E_PNC in cesium.

</details>


### [160] [Fermi gas of domain-wall Skyrmions in QCD in a strong magnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26608)
*Patrick Copinger,Minoru Eto,Muneto Nitta*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析了手征孤子晶格中畴壁Skyrmion的电磁屏蔽特性，发现零温下Skyrmion密度在垂直于磁场的圆盘上饱和至费米动量，并预测了有限温度下通过戴比质量/长度组合实现的静态费米畴壁Skyrmion屏蔽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 畴壁Skyrmion作为手征微扰论中磁场的基态，与量子色动力学的重子相关联。理解其电磁屏蔽特性有助于揭示磁场和化学势相图中的重子密度分布，从而加深对强相互作用物质在极端条件下行为的认识。

Method: 采用模量有效理论在零温下分析Skyrmion密度分布；通过手征微扰论耦合量子电动力学研究有限温度屏蔽效应，利用戴比质量/长度组合描述静态费米畴壁Skyrmion的电磁屏蔽。

Result: 零温下发现Skyrmion密度在垂直于磁场的手征孤子晶格圆盘上饱和至费米动量；有限温度下预测了静态费米畴壁Skyrmion的屏蔽效应，通过戴比质量/长度组合予以表征。

Conclusion: 该研究为磁场与化学势相图中重子密度的电磁探测提供了理论框架，揭示了畴壁Skyrmion的屏蔽特性与重子结构的深层联系。

Abstract: Fermionic domain-wall Skyrmions arise in the chiral soliton lattice of two flavor chiral perturbation theory in a magnetic field as the ground state and are associated with baryons of the underlying theory of quantum chromodynamics. We analyze the electromagnetic screening characteristics of domain-wall Skyrmions that give insight into baryon density on the magnetic field and chemical potential phase diagram. At zero temperature, using the moduli effective theory, it is found Skyrmion density is saturated up to the Fermi momenta on the disk of the chiral soliton lattice orthogonal to the magnetic field. We further study chiral perturbation theory coupled to quantum electrodynamics leading to finite temperature screening, wherein a static fermionic domain-wall Skyrmion screening is predicted through a combined Debye mass/length.

</details>


### [161] [Post-sphaleron conversion between baryon and lepton numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26670)
*Pei-Hong Gu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现，在传统的电弱sphaleron过程停止有效后，通过与适当的标量及TeV级新物理相结合，重子-轻子转换仍可保持高效，为宇宙物质-反物质不对称性的动态产生提供新机制，同时可解决中子寿命之谜并预言多核子衰变到多轻子的新衰变模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重子数与轻子数之间的转换对宇宙中物质-反物质不对称性的动态产生机制具有重要影响。传统观点认为这种转换源自电弱sphaleron过程，但该过程在低温下不再活跃，因此需要探索新的高效转换机制。

Method: 通过在电弱sphaleron不再活跃之后，引入适当的标量场和其他TeV级新物理，实现重子-轻子转换的持续高效进行。该方案同时被应用于解释中子寿命之谜，并预言了多核子到多轻子的衰变模式。

Result: 发现一种新型的重子-轻子转换机制，该机制在电弱sphaleron过程失效后仍能保持高效；该方案可提供对中子寿命之谜的解决方案，并预言了多核子到多轻子的新衰变模式。

Conclusion: 通过引入合适的标量场和TeV级新物理，可以实现电弱sphaleron失效后的持续重子-轻子转换，这不仅为物质-反物质不对称性的产生提供了新途径，还对中子寿命之谜给出了解答并产生了可检验的多核子衰变预言。

Abstract: The conversion between baryon and lepton numbers has a significant impact on the mechanisms for dynamically generating the matter-antimatter asymmetry in the present universe. Under the traditional wisdom, such conversion originates from the so-called electroweak sphaleron processes. In this work we find that in association with a proper scalar and the other TeV new physics, the baryon-lepton conversion can still keep efficient after the electroweak sphalerons are no longer active. Moreover this scenario can provide a solution to the puzzle of neutron lifetime and predict some decay modes of multi-nucleons to multi-leptons.

</details>


### [162] [From Thermal History to Multi-Messenger Signatures in $\mathbb{Z}_3$ Symmetric Dark Sector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26682)
*Debajyoti Choudhury,Jaydeb Das,Divya Sachdeva*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个基于Z3对称性的标准模型扩展，引入右手中微子(N_R)、主导暗物质密度的暗费米子(χ)和促进强一级电弱相变(SFOEWPT)的暗复标量(S)。通过湮灭、半湮灭和暗区转化过程的共同作用在宽参数空间中实现观测到的遗迹丰度，并研究了包括环诱导直接探测、伽马射线间接探测和引力波信号在内的多信使特征，其中引力波信号处于LISA、BBO和DECIGO等未来空间探测器预计灵敏度范围内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一个能够同时解释暗物质遗迹密度和强一级电弱相变的理论框架，并寻找可被未来实验验证的多信使观测特征。

Method: 构建Z3对称的标准模型扩展，包含右手中微子、暗费米子和暗复标量三个新场，通过湮灭、半湮灭和暗区转化机制计算遗迹丰度，并分析直接探测（环诱导）、间接探测（伽马射线）和引力波信号等多信使观测。

Result: 在满足SFOEWPT的宽参数空间中实现了观测到的暗物质遗迹丰度；暗物质直接探测信号为环诱导的，伽马射线提供了间接探测约束；引力波信号落入LISA、BBO和DECIGO的未来空间探测器灵敏度范围内。

Conclusion: 该Z3对称模型能够同时满足强一级电弱相变和暗物质遗迹密度要求，并通过多信使渠道提供可检验的预测，特别是引力波信号为未来空间探测器提供了明确的观测前景。

Abstract: We investigate a $\mathbb{Z}_3$-symmetric extension of the Standard Model consisting of a right-handed neutrino ($N_R$), a dark fermion ($χ$) that dominates the relic density and a dark complex scalar ($S$) that facilitates a strong first-order electroweak phase transition (SFOEWPT). The observed relic abundance, is achieved through the combined effects of annihilation, semi-annihilation, and dark-sector conversion processes over a broad region of parameter space consistent with a SFOEWPT. We further investigate the resulting multi-messenger signatures, including loop-induced direct detection, indirect detection through gamma-ray observations, and gravitational wave signals, with the latter lying within the projected sensitivities of future space-based detectors such as LISA, BBO, and DECIGO.

</details>


### [163] [$μ$DM: a new mechanism for the baryon-dark matter coincidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26709)
*Wen Yin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一类新型暗物质模型——化学势匹配暗物质（μDM），通过使重子数化学势 |μ_B| 与暗物质质量 m_DM 同量级，建立重子-暗物质丰度巧合的新机制。作为具体示例，考虑Affleck-Dine场的激发作为暗物质，并简要讨论与轴子类粒子相关的自发电弱重子产生。无熵稀释时该机制指向eV-keV量级暗物质，但通过玻色增强受激发射可实现冷动量分布；若有熵稀释则可支持至MeV量级质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重子与暗物质的丰度巧合（重子-暗物质质量密度相近）是粒子宇宙学中的核心未解之谜。现有机制（如非热产生、不对称暗物质等）虽能解释某些情形，但缺乏统一而自然的框架。本文旨在提供一条新的途径：化学势匹配机制，将重子数与暗物质质量通过化学势建立直接联系。

Method: 1) 提出化学势匹配暗物质（μDM）概念：利用相对论退耦热遗迹关系 ρ_DM/s ~ 10^{-3} m_DM 与弱sphaleron冻结后重子不对称 ρ_baryon/s ~ 10^{-4}|μ_B| 的类比，令 |μ_B| ~ m_DM 实现丰度巧合。2) 以Affleck-Dine场的激发作为暗物质具体实现该构想。3) 讨论轴子类粒子（ALP）墙诱导的自发电弱重子产生作为补充。4) 分析无熵稀释与有熵稀释两种情形下的暗物质质量范围及动量分布特征（玻色增强受激发射可产生冷分布）。

Result: 该机制在无熵稀释时自然指向eV-keV质量的暗物质，能同时满足重子-暗物质丰度巧合；若暗物质热产生由玻色增强受激发射主导，其动量分布仍可为冷的。当存在熵稀释时，允许暗物质质量提升至MeV量级。

Conclusion: 化学势匹配暗物质提供了一种新颖而有机的重子-暗物质巧合解决方案，将暗物质质量与重子化学势而非通常的粒子物理质量标度相联系，为未来暗物质搜索与电弱物理交叉研究开辟了新方向。

Abstract: For a relativistically decoupled thermal relic, the dark-matter energy-to-entropy ratio scales as $ρ_{\rm DM}/s\sim 10^{-3}m_{\rm DM}$. This is the familiar hot-dark-matter abundance relation. Immediately after weak-sphaleron freeze-out, the baryon asymmetry can be parametrized as $ρ_{\rm baryon}/s\sim 10^{-4}|μ_B|$. Here $μ_B$ is the baryon-number chemical potential. We propose a new class of models, called chemical-potential-matched dark matter ($μ$DM), in which $|μ_B|\sim m_{\rm DM}$, thereby providing a new route to the baryon-dark matter coincidence. As a concrete example of $μ$DM, we consider an Affleck-Dine field whose excitations constitute dark matter. We also briefly discuss spontaneous electroweak baryogenesis associated with axion-like-particle walls. In the absence of entropy dilution, this coincidence mechanism generically points to dark matter in the eV-keV mass range. Its momentum distribution can nevertheless be cold if thermal production is dominated by Bose-enhanced stimulated emission. Alternatively, entropy dilution after both the dark-matter and baryon yields are fixed allows masses up to the MeV scale.

</details>


### [164] [Simulations and flavour-scheme studies for Higgs-boson production in association with charm quarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26863)
*Tiziano Bevilacqua,Christian Biello,Lea Michela Caminada,Clemens Lange,Marino Missiroli,Davide Pagani,Michele Selvaggi,Marco Zaro*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文对Higgs玻色子与charm和bottom夸克联合产生过程进行了详细的NLO+PS研究，重点聚焦H+c终态建模。系统比较了有质量与无质量味道方案，量化了标度和味道方案不确定性，并评估了干涉和环诱导贡献。特别针对4味道方案下的cc̄H产生给出了NLO+PS与NNLO+PS预测的首个对比（13.6 TeV），并首次为LHC分析中cc̄H产生的模拟提供了实用建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Higgs与charm/bottom夸克联合产生是研究Higgs-Yukawa耦合和区分味道方案的重要过程，但H+c终态的建模仍存在不确定性，缺乏系统的比较和实验模拟指导，因此需要详细的NLO+PS研究并提供实用的模拟推荐。

Method: 在NLO+PS精度下系统比较有质量（4FS）和无质量（5FS）味道方案对H+c和H+b产生过程的预测；量化标度不确定性和味道方案间的不确定性；评估干涉效应和环诱导贡献的影响；特别对charm Yukawa聚变产生的cc̄H过程，将NLO+PS预测与4FS下的NNLO+PS计算在13.6 TeV上进行对比。

Result: 提供了H+c和H+b过程在不同味道方案下的系统比较结果，量化了各种理论不确定性来源，评估了干涉和环诱导效应的影响，并首次给出了cc̄H产生在4FS下NLO+PS与NNLO+PS预测的对比结果。

Conclusion: 基于对比结果，首次提出了LHC分析中用于模拟cc̄H产生的实用推荐，为该过程在实验中的精确建模提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We present a detailed NLO+PS study of Higgs-boson production in association with charm and bottom quarks, with particular focus on the modelling of the H+c final state. We systematically compare predictions in massive and massless flavour schemes, quantify scale and flavour-scheme uncertainties, and assess the impact of interference and loop-induced contributions. Special emphasis is placed on the Higgs production via charm Yukawa fusion, for which we compare NLO+PS predictions with a NNLO+PS calculation of $c\bar{c}H$ production in the four-flavour scheme at 13.6 TeV. Based on these results, we provide the first practical recommendations for the simulation of $c\bar{c}H$ production in LHC analyses.

</details>


### [165] [Advances in the holographic description of hot and dense QCD matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27023)
*Nicolas Kovensky,Andreas Schmitt*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述并扩展了规范-引力对偶框架下QCD-like相在非零温度和化学势下的研究进展，聚焦于Witten-Sakai-Sugimoto模型（一个基于弦论的自上而下方法），实现了介子、重子和夸克的统一处理。论文确定了相图中的π凝聚、重子物质和夸克子物质等相，并首次构建了BEC与BCS区域之间的介子相（但该相是亚稳态的），同时讨论了该全息方法的预测、局限及未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QCD在非零温度和化学势下的相结构（如致密核物质和夸克物质相）难以通过微扰QCD或格点QCD完全处理，需要新的理论工具。规范-引力对偶（弦论/全息方法）提供了一种互补的自上而下途径，能够统一描述强耦合区域中的介子、重子和夸克。本文旨在综述并推进这一方向，特别是填补介子相（BEC-BCS过渡区域）的空白。

Method: 基于Witten-Sakai-Sugimoto模型（顶级弦论构造的规范-引力对偶模型），通过全息方法系统地研究QCD-like相在非零温度和化学势下的行为。该模型将介子、重子和夸克统一在弦论框架内处理。本文首次构建了介于BEC（玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚）和BCS（巴丁-库珀-施里弗）区域之间的介子相，并将其定位在相图中。

Result: 论文确定了包含π凝聚、重子物质和夸克子物质在内的完整相图。首次构建的全息介子相（BEC-BCS过渡区）被证明是亚稳态的。此外，论文系统总结了该模型的预测能力及其固有的弱点和局限性。

Conclusion: 全息QCD（特别是Witten-Sakai-Sugimoto模型）为探索QCD相图提供了有价值的补充工具，但也存在固有局限。亚稳态介子相的存在暗示真实的BEC-BCS过渡可能比该模型所捕捉的更为复杂。未来需要在更广泛的上下文和模型中进一步推进全息QCD研究。

Abstract: We review and extend recent progress on QCD-like phases at nonzero temperature and chemical potentials within the gauge-gravity duality. We focus on the Witten-Sakai-Sugimoto model, a top-down approach based on string theory which allows for a unified treatment of mesons, baryons and quarks. Phases such as pion condensation, baryonic and quarkyonic matter are located in the phase diagram, and we discuss predictions as well as weaknesses and limitations of this holographic approach. One of the novelties included in this review is the first holographic construction of a pionic phase between BEC and BCS regimes, which however is found to be metastable. We also discuss future directions in holographic QCD, within the model used here and in a wider context.

</details>


### [166] [Learning Transverse Momentum Distributions from Raw Scattering Events via Conditional Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27077)
*Jitao Xu,Christopher Cocuzza,Kevin Braga,Daniel Lersch,Nobuo Sato,Yaohang Li*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种条件扩散模型，直接从SIDIS事件运动学映射到TMD PDF，绕过传统参数化拟合方法，在CLAS12模拟数据上验证了其有效性和不确定性量化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从SIDIS数据中提取TMD PDF是Jefferson Lab和未来EIC核子结构计划的核心目标。传统提取方法依赖参数化函数形式和迭代拟合，限制了分布灵活性且不确定性量化困难。

Method: 提出条件扩散模型，学习从原始SIDIS事件运动学直接映射到TMD PDF的隐空间，不依赖显式函数假设。模型在CLAS12运动学条件下的模拟SIDIS数据上进行评估。

Result: 模型成功恢复底层TMD分布，提供信息量丰富的不确定性，且随事件统计量增加不确定度稳步收窄；即使在仅1000个条件事件的统计受限区间也能产生可靠估计。

Conclusion: 该条件扩散模型为TMD PDF提取提供了一种无需显式函数形式的柔性替代方案，特别适用于统计量受限的实验情境，对正在进行和计划中的实验具有直接参考价值。

Abstract: Extracting transverse momentum dependent parton distribution functions (TMD PDFs) from semi-inclusive deep inelastic scattering (SIDIS) data is a central goal of the nucleon structure program at Jefferson Lab and the future Electron-Ion Collider. Traditional extraction methods rely on parameterized functional forms and iterative fitting, which can limit the flexibility of the resulting distributions and make uncertainty quantification cumbersome. We present a conditional diffusion model that learns to map raw SIDIS event kinematics directly to TMD PDFs, bypassing explicit functional assumptions. Evaluated on simulated SIDIS data at CLAS12 kinematics, the model recovers the underlying TMD with informative uncertainties that narrow steadily with increasing event statistics, and produces reliable estimates even with as few as 1,000 conditioning events, a statistics-limited regime directly relevant to ongoing and planned experiments.

</details>


### [167] [Too good to go: Upcycling Phase-Space Points for Multijet Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27104)
*Konrad Helms,Timo Janßen,Steffen Schumann*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用相空间嵌套结构的高维相空间采样训练策略。该方法通过将N粒子相空间的(已优化)采样器扩展到(N+1)粒子数据集，为训练样本提供更接近被积函数的初始化分布，从而降低蒙特卡洛事件生成器中采样器的适应成本，并提升其最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维相空间的蒙特卡洛采样是粒子物理事件生成器中的核心挑战，尤其是高多重数末态中散射矩阵元的计算代价高昂。现有的适应策略需要大量矩阵元评估才能训练出高效采样器，计算成本成为瓶颈。作者希望大幅降低采样器适应过程的成本，同时提升采样器的最终性能。

Method: 该方法利用(N+1)粒子相空间可分解为一个N粒子相空间和一个单粒子相空间的嵌套结构。假设N粒子相空间已有高效采样器（例如QCD X+n-jet过程堆栈中的情形），通过将N粒子数据增强为(N+1)粒子数据集，获得统计量更大且更接近真实被积函数的训练样本。训练时以N粒子核心作为初始建议分布，加速(N+1)粒子相空间密度的学习。由于增强样本仅作为初始提议分布，通过精确事件加权（exact event weighting）保证蒙特卡洛估计无偏。该方法与具体采样器无关，适用于基于机器学习的采样器和传统算法（如VEGAS）。

Result: 作者使用连续归一化流（Continuous Normalising Flow）采样器，在LHC的jet伴随Drell-Yan过程和top对产生过程中验证了该方法。结果表明矩阵元评估次数显著减少，同时最终采样性能优于当前基准。

Conclusion: 该方法通过充分利用相空间的嵌套分解结构，以低成本获得高质量的训练样本，显著提升了高维相空间采样的效率。该策略通用性强，可为机器学习采样器和传统采样算法同时带来性能改进，适用于LHC等场景下的高多重数事件生成任务。

Abstract: The efficient sampling of high-dimensional phase spaces is a major challenge for Monte Carlo event generators, as for high-multiplicity final states the evaluation of scattering matrix elements becomes computationally expensive. We here introduce a training strategy that significantly reduces the cost of adapting the samplers, while delivering samplers that outperform the current benchmarks. The method exploits the nested structure of phase spaces, where an $(N+1)$-particle phase space factorises into an $N$-particle and a one-particle phase space. We thereby assume that an efficient sampler for the corresponding $N$-particle phase space is already available, as is the case in stacks of QCD $X+n$-jets processes. By augmenting an $N$-particle to an $(N+1)$-particle dataset, we obtain a training sample that more closely resembles the integrand and is statistically larger than, for example, a uniformly sampled one. Starting the adaptation phase of the sampler with a well-sampled $N$-particle core accelerates learning of the full $(N+1)$-particle phase-space density. Since the augmented sample is only used as an initial proposal distribution, unbiased Monte Carlo estimates are still guaranteed by exact event weighting. The approach is agnostic to the trained sampler and can be applied to machine-learning-based methods as well as more traditional algorithms such as VEGAS. We demonstrate the reduction in matrix-element evaluations and final performance increase for jet-associated Drell--Yan and top-pair production at the LHC, using Continuous Normalising Flow samplers.

</details>


### [168] [Exploring $Z/γ$-mediated heavy FCNCs at the FCC-ee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27183)
*Abhik Sarkar,Subhajit Kala,Amir Subba,Yu Shi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了未来正负电子对撞机FCC-ee上，通过SMEFT中的偶极子和希格斯流算符探测第三代与第一、二代之间的味破缺跃迁（由Z/γ中性规范玻色子介导），并利用最优观测量技术（OOT）在不同质心能量下确定最优灵敏度，同时结合低能味观测量和重费米子衰变通道给出互补约束。结果表明FCC-ee能作为电弱能标下味破缺相互作用的直接且互补的探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型中的味结构仍是粒子物理中最引人注目的未解问题之一，特别是第三代费米子因其显著更大的质量和相对不够精确测量的性质而格外有趣。第三代与第一、二代之间的味跃迁为寻找超越标准模型的新物理提供了独特窗口，因此有必要系统评估未来对撞机在这些通道上的探测能力。

Method: 在SMEFT框架内，利用偶极子和希格斯流算符研究由Z/γ介导的第三代与前两代之间的味破缺跃迁；采用最优观测量技术（OOT）计算FCC-ee在不同质心能量阶段的灵敏度；并从低能味破缺观测量和重费米子衰变通道推导SMEFT算符的互补约束；同时分析偶极子贡献在不同味跃迁和不同能量阶段（Z极点vs高能阶段）的干涉模式。

Result: FCC-ee对多种味破缺相互作用展现出改进的灵敏度，对另一些相互作用的灵敏度则与现有味约束相当；偶极子贡献在不同味跃迁间表现出特征性的干涉模式，且在Z极点与更高能FCC-ee阶段呈现不同的行为。

Conclusion: FCC-ee提供了一种直接且互补的电弱能标味破缺相互作用探测途径。系统评估不同能量阶段（FCC-ee各阶段）非常重要，它能全面揭示该对撞机探索味破缺现象的潜力，并展示其与低能味实验的互补性。

Abstract: The flavor structure of the Standard Model (SM) remains one of the most compelling questions in particle physics, with the third generation being particularly intriguing due to its significantly larger masses and comparatively less precisely measured properties. These features make third-generation flavor transitions particularly interesting in context of search for physics beyond the SM. In this work, we investigate flavor-violating transitions between the third and the first two generations, mediated by the neutral gauge bosons ($Z/γ$), within the framework of the SM Effective Field Theory (SMEFT), using dipole and Higgs-current operators. We determine the optimal sensitivities using the optimal observable technique (OOT) at different center-of-mass energies of the upcoming Future Circular Collider in the $e^+e^-$ mode (FCC-ee). We further derive complementary constraints on the relevant SMEFT operators from low-energy flavor-violating observables and heavy fermion decay channels. Our analysis also reveals characteristic interference patterns among the dipole contributions, which depend on the underlying flavor transition and exhibit distinct behavior between the $Z$ pole and higher-energy FCC-ee stages. The FCC-ee provides a complementary and direct probe of flavor-violating interactions at the electroweak scale, with the projections showing improved sensitivity for several interactions and comparable sensitivity to existing flavor constraints for several others. This highlights the importance of a systematic assessment across the different FCC-ee energy stages, which provides a comprehensive picture of its potential to explore flavor-violating phenomena and its complementarity with low-energy flavor experiments.

</details>


### [169] [Enigmatic properties of $Ξ(1620)$ and $Ξ(1690)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27236)
*Taísa Veloso,K. P. Khemchandani,A. Martinez Torres,Luciano M. Abreu,Li-Sheng Geng*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在耦合道框架下研究粲奇重子Ξ(1620)和Ξ(1690)附近的介子-重子相互作用，试图同时描述K^-Λ费米子关联函数数据以及πΞ、K̄Σ和K̄Λ的不变质量分布数据，但发现两种数据无法自洽同时描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来多个实验合作组发布了奇异重子Ξ(1620)和Ξ(1690)前两个激发态的大量数据，为在这些共振态附近系统研究介子-重子相互作用提供了动力和实验约束。

Method: 采用耦合道方法研究由赝标介子和矢量介子组成的介子-重子系统，针对总自旋1/2和同位旋1/2的构型进行计算。树级相互作用由基于手征对称性和隐局域对称性的拉格朗日量推导得出。

Result: 发现能够较好描述K^-Λ费米子关联函数数据的散射振幅，会导致不同介子-重子系统（πΞ、K̄Σ和K̄Λ）的质量分布与实验数据不一致；反之亦然。即两种类型的实验数据无法同时被同一振幅自洽描述。

Conclusion: K^-Λ费米子关联函数的数据与各介子-重子通道的不变质量分布数据之间存在张力，目前基于手征和隐局域对称性的耦合道模型无法同时协调这两类实验观测。

Abstract: Motivated by a considerable release of data on first two excited states of the doubly strange baryon, $Ξ(1620)$ and $Ξ(1690)$, from different experimental collaborations in recent years, we investigate meson-baryon interactions in a coupled-channel approach. The systems are composed of pseudoscalar and vector mesons and the calculations are done for the total spin 1/2 and isospin 1/2 configuration. The tree-level interactions are deduced from Lagrangians based on chiral and hidden local symmetries. Specifically, we make an attempt to describe (1) data on the $K^-Λ$ femtoscopic correlation function coming from $p\,p$ and $Pb\,Pb$ collisions, and (2) the available data on invariant mass distributions for different systems: $πΞ$, $\bar K Σ$ and $\bar K Λ$. We find that the amplitude which yields the correlation function close to the data results in mass distributions of different meson-baryon systems that are in discordance with the experimental data and vice-versa.

</details>


### [170] [Hunting long-lived doubly charged scalars at the HL-LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27251)
*Biplob Bhattacherjee,Rituparna Ghosh,Swagata Mukherjee,Nabin Kumar Pidikaka*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究长寿命双电荷标量粒子的对撞机现象学，指出现有搜索方案在中间衰变长度区间（cτ约为0.1-100 mm）存在灵敏度空白，并提出在HL-LHC上利用位移顶点搜索来探测该粒子，覆盖质量最高达约1 TeV的费米子phobic场景和120 GeV的耦合场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双电荷标量粒子在即时衰变和长寿命两种极端区域已有搜索，但在中间范围的衰变长度（cτ约0.1-100 mm）内，传统搜索方法失去灵敏度，存在未被探索的参数空间。作者旨在填补这一搜索空白。

Method: 作者首先回顾了双电荷标量在即时和长寿命两类物理情境下的现有搜索，分析其灵敏度覆盖范围。随后考虑有/无ΔL=2汤川耦合两种场景，提出HL-LHC上的位移顶点搜索策略，通过重建位移顶点的关联径迹不变量质量强有力地压低标准模型背景，并给出在√s=14 TeV、3000 fb⁻¹积分亮度下的Drell-Yan对产生截面投影极限。

Result: 在有ΔL=2耦合的情况下，双电荷标量仅能在100-150 GeV质量范围内实现长寿命；在费米子phobic（无ΔL=2耦合）场景下，该范围可扩展至TeV标度。提出的位移顶点搜索方案可在费米子phobic场景下覆盖质量达约1 TeV（cτ=10-100 mm），以及120 GeV质量、cτ=5 mm的基准点。不变量质量切割能有效压低标准模型背景。

Conclusion: 位移顶点搜索能够探测即时衰变搜索和重稳定带电粒子搜索所覆盖区域之外的互补参数空间，为未来HL-LHC实验寻找双电荷标量粒子提供了新的可行途径。

Abstract: This work studies the collider phenomenology of long-lived doubly charged scalars. After reviewing the existing searches for the doubly charged scalar in both the prompt and the long-lived regimes, we identify an intermediate range of proper decay length, namely, $\mathcal{O}(0.1~\mathrm{mm}) \lesssim cτ\lesssim \mathcal{O}(100~\mathrm{mm})$, where conventional searches lose sensitivity. We investigate the prospects for probing the doubly charged scalar both in the presence and in the absence of the $ΔL=2$ Yukawa coupling of the $SU(2)_{L}$ complex triplet. In the presence of this coupling, $H^{\pm\pm}$ can be long-lived only for masses in the range $100$-$150~\mathrm{GeV}$, whereas in the fermiophobic scenario (i.e., in the absence of the $ΔL=2$ coupling), the range extends to $\mathrm{TeV}$ scale. We propose a displaced-vertex search at the HL-LHC for doubly charged scalars with masses up to $\approx 1~\mathrm{TeV}$ and $cτ=10$-$100~\mathrm{mm}$ in the fermiophobic scenario, while a benchmark point with a doubly charged scalar of mass $120~\mathrm{GeV}$ and $cτ= 5~\mathrm{mm}$ is considered when the complex triplet scalar couples to leptons. We show that a cut on the invariant mass of the displaced vertex as reconstructed from the associated tracks can strongly suppress Standard Model backgrounds. We present projected limits on the Drell-Yan pair-production cross section for the doubly charged scalar at $\sqrt{s}=14~\mathrm{TeV}$ with an integrated luminosity of $3000~\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$, considering two illustrative assumptions for the residual background. Displaced-vertex searches thus probe a region complementary to those covered by prompt and heavy stable charged-particle searches.

</details>


### [171] [An Axial $U_A(1)_{L_μ-L_τ}$: UV Completion and Experimental Searches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27258)
*Rundong Fang,Jinhui Guo,Ming Li,Jia Liu,Xiao-Ping Wang,Yiheng Xiong*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个无反常且可重整化的轴向U_A(1)_{L_μ-L_τ}模型，通过普通级联跷跷板机制产生μ子和τ子的质量，系统研究了A'玻色子从MeV到TeV质量尺度的实验信号，并在(m_{A'}, g_X)参数平面上给出了综合实验约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的L_μ-L_τ模型在解释muon反常磁矩和轻子味物理方面受到关注，但其通常是无轴向耦合的矢量模型。本文旨在探索一种轴向版本的L_μ-L_τ模型，该模型无反常且可重整化，并系统评估其唯象学可行性及实验可探测性。

Method: 构建无反常、可重整化的轴向U_A(1)_{L_μ-L_τ}模型：左右手带电轻子带相反电荷以禁止普通Yukawa耦合，质量通过单态标量与重矢量轻子经由普通级联跷跷板机制产生。选取重矢量轻子、小轻-重混合及m_s≳10 GeV的基准参数空间，使可观测量主要依赖于(m_{A'}, g_X)，并对比中微子三重产生、muon轫致辐射、(g-2)_μ、不可见介子衰变和四μ子共振搜索等实验限制。

Result: 该模型中A'修改的是轴向弱系数而非传统L_μ-L_τ模型中的矢量系数；纵模增强了muon轫致辐射并对(g-2)_μ给出负贡献且在该基准下主导标量贡献。当m_{A'}≫m_μ时，矢量与轴向末态辐射速率几乎相同，可通过速率匹配获得对撞机限制。未来μ子对撞机中，总速率无法完全分辨相互作用结构，但角分布（特别是μ^+μ^-→τ^+τ^-的前后不对称性）对手征性保持直接灵敏度。

Conclusion: 该模型在(m_{A'}, g_X)平面上给出了综合唯象学约束图景；轴向结构在中微子三重产生和(g-2)_μ方面与标准L_μ-L_τ模型有本质区别；未来muon对撞机的角分布观测是区分该轴向模型与普通矢量模型的关键。

Abstract: We propose an anomaly-free and renormalizable axial $U_A(1)_{L_μ-L_τ}$ model and study its experimental signatures for $A'$ masses from the MeV scale to the TeV scale. The opposite charges of the left- and right-handed charged leptons forbid the usual muon and tau Yukawa interactions. Their masses are instead generated by a singlet scalar and heavy vector-like leptons through a universal-seesaw mechanism. We focus on heavy vector-like leptons, small light--heavy mixing, and $m_s\gtrsim10~\mathrm{GeV}$. In this limit, the observables considered here depend mainly on $(m_{A'},g_X)$, while the other model parameters are restricted by mixing and perturbativity. We confront this benchmark with current experimental searches. For neutrino trident production, our finite-$m_μ$ calculation shows that $A'$ modifies the axial weak coefficient, rather than the vector coefficient relevant to the usual $L_μ-L_τ$ model. The longitudinal mode enhances muon bremsstrahlung and gives a negative contribution to $(g-2)_μ$; the latter dominates over the scalar contribution in our benchmark. Combining these results with invisible meson decays and four-muon resonance searches, we summarize the phenomenological constraints in the $(m_{A'},g_X)$ plane. For $m_{A'}\gg m_μ$, vector and axial final-state-radiation rates become nearly identical, so the corresponding collider limits can be obtained by rate matching. At a future muon collider, the total rate alone does not fully resolve the interaction structure, whereas angular distributions, especially the forward--backward asymmetry in $μ^+μ^-\toτ^+τ^-$, retain direct sensitivity to chirality.

</details>


### [172] [From the November Revolution toward the Millennium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27279)
*Chris Quigg*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文是第四届粒子物理学史国际研讨会的主旨演讲，回顾了从J/ψ发现之前到1980年代初的粒子物理学发展历程，为后续研讨会报告提供背景脉络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为第四届粒子物理学史国际研讨会提供开幕性综述，梳理1980-2000年间粒子物理学发展的历史背景，使与会者更好地理解后续专题报告的历史语境。

Method: 以主旨演讲（keynote lecture）的形式，采用历史叙事方法，从J/ψ发现的前史开始，系统回顾至1980年代初的粒子物理学关键发展。

Result: 该文是一篇综述性主旨报告，覆盖了从J/ψ发现之前到1980年代初的粒子物理发展历程，为研讨会后续的专题讨论提供了历史框架和语境。

Conclusion: 该主旨演讲旨在为研讨会参与者提供粒子物理学黄金发展期的历史全景，帮助理解后续报告的历史坐标，并促进对粒子物理学这段关键历史的深入探讨。

Abstract: The 4th International Symposium on the History of Particle Physics spanned two decades, from approximately 1980 to 2000. In this opening keynote lecture, I survey developments in particle physics from the prehistory of the $J/ψ$ discovery to the early nineteen-eighties. I offer context for the contributions that follow in the symposium program.

</details>


### [173] [Chiral soliton lattice in inhomogeneous magnetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27319)
*Tomas Brauner,Ramkumar Radhakrishnan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究非均匀磁场下量子色动力学（QCD）中中性π介子凝聚形成手性孤子晶格（CSL）的可能性。通过低能有效场论，在无质量π介子极限下完整刻画了支持CSL态的非均匀磁场，并对无限族磁场解析求解了CSL态；在有限π介子质量情形下则进行数值最小化。结论表明，虽然磁场弯曲会减小中性π凝聚的能量增益，CSL型基态仍然可能存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明，在足够强的均匀磁场下，QCD基态支持中性π介子的空间调制凝聚（手性孤子晶格CSL）。然而实际应用场景（如重离子碰撞和中子星）中的磁场往往是非均匀的，因此需要探究非均匀磁场下是否仍可能存在类似的CSL有序基态。

Method: 使用QCD低能有效场论，在强磁场下仅保留中性π介子作为低能自由度。在π介子无质量极限下对支撑CSL态的非均匀磁场进行完整分类，并对特定的无限族磁场解析求解CSL态。对有限π介子质量情形，通过能量泛函的全数值最小化获取基态，并与均匀磁场和无质量极限情形进行定性对比。

Result: 在无质量极限下完整刻画了支持CSL基态的非均匀磁场类别，并解析求得了无限族磁场所对应的CSL态；在有限质量情形下提供了示例数值结果，展示了与均匀磁场及无质量极限的定性差异。主要结论为：磁场弯曲通常减少中性π凝聚带来的能量增益，但CSL型基态仍可持续存在。此外，附带给出了均匀磁场有限体积下QCD相图中CSL相的位置。

Conclusion: 非均匀磁场下CSL型有序基态仍然可能存在，尽管弯曲磁场倾向于降低凝聚的能量收益；该结果表明CSL现象在重离子碰撞和中子星等非均匀磁场环境中可能具有实际相关性。

Abstract: It has been known that in sufficiently strong uniform magnetic fields, the ground state of quantum chromodynamics (QCD) supports a spatially modulated condensate of neutral pions, dubbed chiral soliton lattice (CSL). In this paper, we investigate whether a similar ordered ground state might exist when the external magnetic field is nonuniform, as appropriate for potential phenomenological applications, including heavy-ion collisions and neutron stars. To that end, we use the low-energy effective field theory of QCD, restricted to the neutral pions as the sole low-energy degrees of freedom in strong magnetic fields. In the limit of vanishing pion mass, we achieve a complete characterization of magnetic fields supporting a CSL-like ground state. Moreover, for a simple but infinite family of magnetic fields, we find the corresponding CSL state analytically. Going away from the massless limit requires full numerical minimization of the energy functional. Here we provide some sample numerical results, focusing on the qualitative differences as compared to the situations with a uniform magnetic field or a vanishing pion mass. The main conclusion remains unchanged: while bending the magnetic field typically reduces the energy gain due to the neutral pion condensation, a CSL-type ground state is still possible. As a byproduct of our work, we map the location of the CSL phase in the phase diagram of QCD in a uniform magnetic field and finite volume.

</details>
